### E — chooser copy (Council 2) — ✅ `1.4.8-rc`
- `DivideChooserSheet.tsx` + `divideChooser.*` (pt/en/es): subtitle is now the **discriminator** ("É uma conta agora, ou várias contas ao longo do tempo?"); each card carries a **scope chip** (agora · 1 conta × contínuo · a viagem toda), a **concrete example** (jantar €80 pra 4 × a viagem toda — casa/carro/mercado), and the group title anchors to **"· tipo Tricount"**. Removed the verbose `bill_desc`/`group_desc` (only the sheet used them; parity kept by swapping for `*_scope`/`*_example` in all three locales). Copy/visual only — both flows untouched.

### B — scrollbar regression hardening — ✅ `1.4.9-rc`
- Julio's report: Android, **old APK installed**, the bar came back. The DEC-331 global lock (`*` + html/body pseudo) was already strong; the uncovered vector is the one the existing `#root` comment names but did NOT cover — the **zoomed native viewport** (`html.cap-native` / its body) on aged Android System WebView, where the document bar is painted in the `zoom` coordinate space and the universal pseudo isn't reliably matched. `globals.css`: added a higher-specificity `html.cap-native::-webkit-scrollbar` + `html.cap-native body::-webkit-scrollbar` hide. `style-hygiene.test.ts`: **+1** lock asserting that rule (so it can't silently regress). **Honest caveat:** couldn't reproduce the actual old-APK WebView here — a stale APK running its own baked-in web layer is only fully cured by a fresh build (Item A); this OTA CSS covers the old-shell-loads-current-web case. No ledger/logic touched (CSS + test only).

### G2 — Images → access-controlled plaintext on R2 (done 2026-06-26) → `1.3.2-rc`  [✅ L-IMG / DEC-348]
> **REVERSAL of the predecessor G5 (DEC-342/343 image E2E).** The predecessor encrypted each shared image with a per-image AES key carried in the E2E payload — but a `/g/` **web guest has no app to decrypt**, photos sometimes **didn't persist** (upload deferred to share-time), and only **one** photo per expense was possible. DEC-348 makes shared images **access-controlled plaintext** on R2: secrecy = an **unguessable `r2Id`** (handed out only inside the still-E2E share blob) + **TTL** + **delete-on-revoke**. **DEC-207 is unchanged — messages/debts/names/statements STAY ciphertext-only; images are the single carve-out.**
- **Domain first (pure, additive).** `ImageRef.key` is now **optional** (`image-ref.ts` + Zod) — a **plaintext** ref omits it; a **legacy** 1.2.4-rc ref still carries it (back-compat read). `GroupExpense` gained `imageRefs?: ImageRef[]` (**F08 multi-photo**); the legacy single `imageRef?` is retained for old rows. New pure helper **`groupExpenseImages(expense)`** (`group-split.ts`, exported) unifies the read: prefer `imageRefs[]`, else wrap the legacy single, else `[]` — the **one** function every surface now calls. `share-payload.ts` + `claim-response.ts` Zod schemas carry `imageRefs[]`; `foldClaimExpense` copies it so a **guest-authored receipt survives the claim** round-trip.
- **Boundary (`data/sync/media-link.ts`).** Deleted the scramble path; added **`uploadImage(blob, dims)`** — PUT the real `image/jpeg` bytes under a random `r2Id` with `Content-Type` + `X-Img-TTL`, returns a **key-less** `ImageRef` (honest early refusal via `checkImageBytes`); **`imageUrl(ref)`** → the direct `/img/:id` URL used as `<img src>`. `fetchDecryptedImageUrl` stays as a **legacy shim** (no-ops — returns `null` — for a ref without a `key`).
- **F07 · persistence (upload on attach).** `GroupExpenseEditor` now uploads **the moment a photo is attached** (not at share-time): `handleAttachPhoto` compresses → `uploadImage` → appends the ref to `imageRefs[]`; `handleSave` writes `imageRefs` onto the expense + drops the legacy `imageRef`; `handleRemovePhoto`/`handleCancel` clean up (delete a just-added blob immediately; queue an existing one for delete-on-save; **delete orphans if the editor is cancelled**). Removed the now-dead `isShared` prop + `GroupExpenseImageIntent` type. Local `EditorThumb` renders each pending/saved thumb (plaintext direct, legacy via the decrypt shim).
- **F06/F09 · surfaces.** `GroupSplitDetailPage` dropped the entire **reconciliation `useEffect`** + `pendingImages`/`staleRefsRef`/`uploadingRef` machinery (the editor owns uploads now); the gallery + the row camera icon iterate `groupExpenseImages(exp)`; **delete-expense and share-revoke now delete every blob** (`deleteSharedImage` over all refs) and strip both `imageRef` **and** `imageRefs`. `GroupClaimPage` (the `/g/` **web-guest** board) renders all `imageRefs` per row via `groupExpenseImages` — **a no-app guest now sees + downloads receipts**. `GroupImage`/`ImageLightbox`: plaintext refs render straight from `imageUrl`; legacy refs decrypt via the shim; download fetches the blob → object URL (reliable cross-origin save).
- **Worker (`worker/src/index.ts`, `handleImg`).** PUT now reads the request `Content-Type`, validates it against an allowlist (`image/jpeg|png|webp|gif` + legacy `application/octet-stream`) and stores it in `httpMetadata`; GET serves that stored content-type (legacy objects fall back to `octet-stream`). **Messages/debts/names relay is untouched — still ciphertext-only.**

### G3 — Live group board (done 2026-06-26) → `1.3.3-rc`  [✅ L-LIVE / DEC-349]
> **REVERSAL of the predecessor owner-as-sync-gate.** The predecessor wave made the **owner the reducer**: a guest contribution only entered the shared board after the owner re-opened the group and re-published. On Julio's device test this surfaced as the **120€ bug** — a guest deleted an expense but the `/g/` board kept showing the old total (the deletion never re-folded for other viewers). DEC-349 makes the board **live**: **every viewer folds all peer snapshots locally**; the owner stays the **money authority + tombstone moderator** (canonical settle + the power to remove), **not** a sync gate. **Ledger-math invariance holds** — the arithmetic (`reduceGroupClaims`) is byte-identical; we fixed *when* truth propagates and *how* often viewers re-fold.
- **Domain first (pure, reuse).** New pure projection **`foldEventForViewer(base, responses)`** in `domain/group-split/claim-response.ts` — a thin wrapper over the existing **`reduceGroupClaims`** (zero new arithmetic), exported from `domain/group-split/index.ts`. It applies **every** guest response snapshot (incl. the viewer's own draft) onto the owner-published base so any viewer sees the same converged board. The owner's tombstones win: a removed expense **cannot be resurrected** by a re-posted guest snapshot (moderator authority).
- **F10 · live board (`GroupClaimPage`).** The `/g/` guest board now also pulls `fetchGroupResponses(id, key)` on every `refetch` and stores them in `responses` state; the render computes `liveEvent = foldEventForViewer(load.payload.event, responses)` and feeds **that** to `ClaimBoard`. The pull is **best-effort** (`try/catch`) — a relay miss degrades to the owner-published base, never an error or a blocked view (never-block-contribution ÂNCORA).
- **F11 · delete recalcs immediately (the 120€ bug).** Because the board re-folds **all** current snapshots on each pull, a guest removing an expense drops it from `responses` → `foldEventForViewer` recomputes `groupTotalCents` + balances live for everyone. Regression-pinned by a unit test that reproduces the exact 120€→correct path.
- **F12 · honest copy (`group_claim`, pt/en/es).** Dropped the dishonest "aguardando o organizador sincronizar" framing: `add_expense_hint` now says the entry **appears for everyone automatically**, and the per-row `pending` chip is **"Enviando…" / "Sending…" / "Enviando…"** (in-flight, not "waiting for the organizer"). No string hard-coded; parity guarded.
- **Tests (+5):** new `domain/group-split/live-read-fold.test.ts` (cross-guest **convergence**; the **120€ delete-recalc** regression; **owner-tombstone wins** over a re-posted snapshot; **idempotent** double-fold — Σ unchanged, **ledger-math invariance**); new `features/group-split/group-claim-live-board.test.tsx` (the page folds a peer's pulled snapshot → the expense **and** the live total render). New `e2e/group-reliability-g3.spec.ts` (owner-side delete → the row + its amount disappear, total recalcs). **Self-check (5-point):** (1) AC F10–F12 done; (2) earlier ACs verified — **ledger-math invariance** (`reduceGroupClaims` reused untouched; asserted idempotent + Σ-stable in the fold tests), **never-block-contribution** (the pull is best-effort; a failure degrades to base, never blocks viewing/posting), **Worker-ciphertext for messages/debts/names** (no Worker touched — Pages-only OTA; the relay stays ciphertext); (3) tests **2410/2412 = +5, 0 new failures**; (4) out-of-scope: none (claim-response domain + index + `GroupClaimPage` + i18n + version/release-notes/dev-log); (5) this entry.
- **Gate close (G3):** unit **2410 pass / 2412** (2 WebCrypto Node-18 baseline only) · `npx tsc --noEmit` clean (app **and** `worker/`) · `npm run build:pages` green (`index` **415.41 KB** < 500 KB; `bundles/1.3.3-rc.zip` 968 KB). Bump 1.3.2-rc→**1.3.3-rc** (package.json + app-version.ts + public/version.json `bundleUrl`→`1.3.3-rc.zip` + RELEASE_NOTES pt/en/es). **Deploy:** commit + push `master` → Cloudflare Pages auto-build (**Pages-only OTA — no Worker diff**); then verify `/version.json` + `/bundles/1.3.3-rc.zip`. ✅ **Verified live `1.3.3-rc`.**

### G4 — Identity name, bilateral connect, Android QR zoom (done 2026-06-26) → `1.3.4-rc`  [✅ DEC-350]
> Julio's field test showed peers appearing as **"Android Chrome"** and a **one-sided** connect (only one phone saw the other). Root cause: the **forward identity QR already** used the onboarding owner name, but the **orchestrators** that stamp every share/connect/P2P envelope hard-coded `settings.deviceName` (the auto device label, DEC-266). DEC-350 (Julio: *"no onboarding já colocamos o nome, então pega o nome do onboarding"*) makes the **active trip's owner participant name** the identity everywhere.
- **Domain first (pure).** New `domain/sync/self-name.ts` — `resolveSelfName({ ownerName, profileName, deviceName })` with the locked precedence **owner ?? profileName ?? deviceName** (each trimmed; whitespace skipped). Zero React/IO → fully unit-tested. `AppSettings` gained an **optional** `profileName?: string | null` (the social-name fallback; **non-indexed, no migration** — `undefined` on existing installs; most users never set it).
- **Orchestrator helper (impure, single source).** `sync-orchestrators.ts` → `resolveSelfShareName(settings?)`: reads the **active trip's** owner participant (`isOwner && !deletedAt`) name, then folds it through `resolveSelfName`. Accepts the already-loaded `settings` to avoid a second read (perf ÂNCORA); `try/catch` so a read failure **never blocks a share** (falls back to the settings name). Exported from the orchestrators barrel.
- **F13 · name everywhere.** Swapped **every** `fromName`/connect-name stamp from `settings.deviceName` → `resolveSelfShareName(settings)`: `mailbox-orchestrators` (`sendPayloadToPeerMailbox`, `sendConnectHandshake`), `p2p-orchestrators` (`sealAndQueue`, `shareDebtWithPeer`, `announcePaymentToPeer` + the two income-transaction descriptions, `confirmInboundPayment`). `backup-orchestrators` `deviceId` stays `deviceName` (the **technical** backup label — correct carve-out).
- **F14 · bilateral connect.** The **reverse `connect` handshake** (`sendConnectHandshake`) now seals **both** the envelope `fromName` and the `ConnectPayload.name` with the **resolved onboarding name** (+ my pubkey, unchanged). All scan entry points already route through `connectPeerFromIdentity`/`linkConnectFromIdentity` (so the handshake always fires) and `upsertPeerLinkFromConnect` upserts the reverse link (dedup by actorId, `participantId: null` preserves any ledger mapping) → **both sides now show the right name**, no "cobrar pelo app" second action. **Ledger-neutral** (only delivery-address + display name written).
- **F15 · Android QR zoom (`QrScanner.tsx`).** Android exposes zoom **only after the track settles**, so the previous synchronous `getCapabilities()` read saw an empty/degenerate range → no zoom buttons. Now: pure `deriveZoomState(capabilities, currentZoom?)` (null on empty/`min===max`; current clamped into range) is re-run **on `loadedmetadata` + retries (250/700/1500 ms)** keeping the first usable range; pure `zoomMode(zoom)` → `presets` (≥2 of 1×/2×/3× fit) / `slider` (camera zooms but presets don't qualify — e.g. `{1,1.6}`) / `none`. The UI shows the preset chips **or a continuous slider** so Android keeps a working zoom. Timers cleared on cleanup.
- **F13 · Settings "Seu nome".** A new `settings.your_name` field (above the device-name field) writes `profileName`, placeholder = the resolved owner name (honest empty state); a hint explains it only **overrides** the trip name. The device-name field gained a hint clarifying it is the **technical/backup** label (hide-never-delete — nothing removed). i18n pt/en/es parity (+`sync.zoom`).
- **Tests (+18):** `domain/sync/self-name.test.ts` (precedence owner→profile→device, trim, empty ×5); `domain/orchestrators/self-share-name.test.ts` (fake-indexeddb — owner wins, no-trip→profile, →device, soft-deleted owner ignored, pre-loaded settings ×5); `components/qr-scanner-zoom.test.ts` extended (+8 — `deriveZoomState` empty/degenerate/real/honour-setting/clamp; `zoomMode` none/presets/slider). **Self-check (5-point):** (1) AC F13–F15 done; (2) earlier ACs verified — **ledger-math invariance** (no amount/balance/transfer math touched — only display name strings; p2p-ledger + group-split suites green), **never-block-contribution** (`resolveSelfShareName` `try/catch`→fallback, never throws; no expense/claim path gated), **Worker-ciphertext for messages/debts/names** (no Worker touched — Pages-only OTA; relay stays ciphertext); (3) tests **2428/2430 = +18, 0 new failures**; (4) out-of-scope: none (self-name domain + app-settings + 3 orchestrators + QrScanner + SettingsPage + i18n + version/release-notes/dev-log); (5) this entry.

### G5 — Every app QR is a URL (done 2026-06-26) → `1.3.5-rc`  [✅ DEC-351]
> Julio's field test: pointing a phone's **default camera** at an in-app QR showed raw `TPSYNC1:…` text (no actionable link). DEC-351 makes **every** app QR a real deep-link URL, so the default camera opens TripPilot (or the web route) directly. Two payloads were still raw: **identity** (the connect QR) and **statement** (the offline settle-up QR). Group invites (`/g/`) and bill-split (`/s/`) were **already** URLs; the **live WebRTC signaling** QRs (session/offer/answer) stay raw — the explicit DEC-351 carve-out (scanned ONLY by the guided in-app scanner; a default camera can't act on a half-handshake).
- **Domain first (pure, single source).** New `domain/sync/qr-url.ts`: **`buildQrUrl(kind, encoded, origin)`** maps `kind → route` via the `QR_URL_ROUTES` table (`identity → /pair`, `statement → /sync`), strips a trailing origin slash, and percent-encodes the envelope into the **`#fragment`** (the host never receives it → same client-only secrecy as `/g/`, `/s/`, `/pair`). **`extractQrEnvelope(text)`** is the tolerant inverse: returns the decoded `#fragment` for a URL, the **bare envelope** when there is no `#` (legacy raw QRs + live-signaling QRs), and `null` only for empty input — so every scanner keeps working through the migration. `pair-link.ts` `pairLinkFromEncoded` now **delegates to `buildQrUrl('identity', …)`** (and `PAIR_PATH = QR_URL_ROUTES.identity`) → one URL builder, zero drift. Exported from the sync barrel.
- **F16 · QR displays → URLs.** `SharedExpensesPage`: the **My-QR** sheet renders `buildQrUrl('identity', myIdentityQr, getShareOrigin())`; the **send-statement** sheet renders `buildQrUrl('statement', statementQrText, getShareOrigin())`. (Share/copy-link already produced `/pair#` via `pairLinkFromEncoded` — now the same builder underneath.)
- **F16 · scanners accept URL *and* raw.** `SharedExpensesPage` `handlePairScan`/`handleLinkScan` and `SyncTransferFlow` `handleScan` now decode `extractQrEnvelope(text) ?? …` before `decodeQrPayload` — a scan of the new URL QR, a legacy raw QR, or a live-signaling QR (no `#`) all resolve. `SyncReceivePage` gained a **one-shot mount effect**: a default-camera open of `/sync#<statement>` decodes the fragment, stores the mirrored statement, clears the fragment (`history.replaceState` — no re-import on back/reload), and lands the user on `/shared`. `/pair#<identity>` was already handled by `PairPage` (`parsePairIdentityFromHash`) — unchanged.
- **Tests (+11):** `domain/sync/qr-url.test.ts` — `buildQrUrl` routes (identity→`/pair`, statement→`/sync`), route-table parity, trailing-slash strip, percent-encode (valid `new URL()`); `extractQrEnvelope` URL-fragment / bare-envelope / whitespace-trim / empty+fragmentless→null / malformed-percent fallback; **round-trip** `buildQrUrl → extractQrEnvelope` per kind + a **real identity envelope** that survives the URL and still `decodeQrPayload`s. **Self-check (5-point):** (1) AC F16 done (identity + statement are URLs; scanners accept both; default-camera deep-links land); (2) earlier ACs verified — **ledger-math invariance** (no amount/balance/transfer math touched — only QR string wrapping; p2p-ledger + group-split suites green), **never-block-contribution** (scanners fall back to raw when there is no `#`; nothing new gates a contribution), **Worker-ciphertext for messages/debts/names** (no Worker touched — payload stays in the client-only `#fragment`; Pages-only OTA); (3) tests **2439/2441 = +11, 0 new failures**; (4) out-of-scope: none (qr-url + pair-link domain + SharedExpensesPage + SyncTransferFlow + SyncReceivePage + version/release-notes/dev-log); (5) this entry.
- **Gate close (G5):** unit **2439 pass / 2441** (2 WebCrypto Node-18 baseline only) · `npx tsc --noEmit` clean (app **and** `worker/`) · `npm run build:pages` green (`index` < 500 KB; `bundles/1.3.5-rc.zip`). Bump 1.3.4-rc→**1.3.5-rc** (package.json + app-version.ts + public/version.json `bundleUrl`→`1.3.5-rc.zip` + RELEASE_NOTES pt/en/es). **Deploy:** commit + push `master` → Cloudflare Pages auto-build (**Pages-only OTA — no Worker diff**); then verify `/version.json` + `/bundles/1.3.5-rc.zip`. DEC-351 → APPROVED.

### G6 — Real-time peer-ping + notifications (HEADLINE, done 2026-06-26) → `1.4.0-rc`  [✅ L-RT / DEC-352]
> **Promotes DEC-344's deferred V2 peer-ping into scope.** Julio's field test: *"a cobrança só apareceu quando recarreguei — tinha que chegar na hora e ficar na central de notificações e na tela inicial."* The async mailbox already DELIVERED sealed P2P blobs; the gap was **latency + surfacing** — a recipient only drained on open/focus, and an inbound charge only became a transient toast + a `/shared` inbox row (never the notification center, the home, or the OS). DEC-352 makes delivery **real-time while the app is reachable** and **durable** (survives a missed toast). **No money math touched — ledger-math invariance holds** (the ping carries no payload; the same accept-first drain folds the same numbers).
- **m1 · peer-ping transport (`data/sync/peer-ping.ts`, reuse).** `pingPeerMailbox(peerActorId)` opens a short socket to the peer's room and flushes one `{t:'p2p'}` frame on connect (best-effort, closed after a 4 s grace — never leaks a socket per send); `subscribePeerPings(myActorId, onPing)` keeps a socket on MY OWN room and runs `onPing` only for a `p2p` poke. **Reuses `connectShareSignal`** (auto-reconnect/backoff/outbox) and the **already-deployed generic `ShareSignal` DO** (`/share/<id>/ws`) — keyed by the peer's `actorId` (a different DO instance than any shareId; no collision). `ShareSignalType` gained `'p2p'` (a tiny extension to the generic relay protocol; `parseSignal` accepts it). The ping says only "drain now" — **content stays E2E-sealed in the mailbox (DEC-207)**.
- **m2 · ping on send + self-subscribe + forced drain.** The ping fires in **`flushOutbox()`** right after a successful `postToMailbox` — the **single chokepoint every P2P send funnels through** (`sendPayloadToPeerMailbox`, `sendConnectHandshake`, p2p `sealAndQueue` → debt/payment), so one change covers all kinds. `mailbox-boot.registerMailboxSync` now opens `subscribePeerPings(myActorId, () => runMailboxSync(true))` (only when `mailboxEnabled`); `runMailboxSync(force=false)` gained `force` so a poke **bypasses the 30 s poll debounce** and drains immediately → `MAILBOX_DRAINED_EVENT` → `/shared` (and the bell/home) refresh with no reload.
- **m3 · notification center (`domain/insights/notifications.ts`, pure).** New `pending_p2p` kind, pushed **first** (a peer waiting is the most time-sensitive), tone warning, destination `/shared`, from `getInboundP2pItems().length`. `useNotifications` fetches the count alongside its other reads and **re-derives on `MAILBOX_DRAINED_EVENT`** (a real-time arrival isn't an appData change). `NotificationsPage` renders it (icon `payments`, group `action`, copy). Derived (no new table) → the item persists in the mailbox inbox until acted on, so it **survives a missed toast**.
- **m4 · native OS notification (`utils/native/notifications.ts`).** New best-effort `showLocalNotification(title, body)` (native + granted-permission only; no-op on web, never throws). `runMailboxSync` fires it once when actionable items (`debts + payments`) arrive (the existing per-type toasts stay). Connect handshakes stay toast-only.
- **m5 · home "pending actions" card (`dashboard-cards.ts` + `DashboardCards.tsx`).** New `pending_p2p` **fixed** card (a self-gating anchor, placed right under the hero) → one-tap to `/shared`. `useDashboardModel` loads the inbound count separately (it lives in the mailbox inbox, not appData) and **refreshes on `MAILBOX_DRAINED_EVENT`**.
- **Tests (+6):** `data/sync/peer-ping.test.ts` (fake WebSocket — ping opens the peer room + flushes `p2p` on open; empty-actor no-op; grace-window close; subscribe fires only on `p2p`, ignores `upd`/`resp`; empty-actor returns null ×5); `notifications.test.ts` (+1 — inbound P2P leads the list, routes to `/shared`); `dashboard-cards.test.ts` updated (the new fixed anchor `pending_p2p` keeps slot 4; movable filters exclude it). **Self-check (5-point):** (1) AC F17–F19 done (charge pops without reload via ping→forced drain; in the center + home + native; closed app still drains on next open; no re-scan); (2) earlier ACs verified — **ledger-math invariance** (no amount/balance/settlement math touched — the ping is payload-less and the same accept-first drain runs; p2p-ledger + splitting suites green), **never-block-contribution** (ping is fire-and-forget `try/catch`; subscribe returns null on failure; nothing new gates a send/contribution), **Worker-ciphertext for messages/debts/names** (NO Worker change — the relay only carries a contentless "drain now" poke; sealed blobs stay E2E, DEC-207 intact); (3) tests **2445/2447 = +6, 0 new failures**; (4) out-of-scope: none (peer-ping + share-signal + mailbox-orch/boot + notifications domain/hook/page + native notif + dashboard card/model/render + i18n + version/release-notes/dev-log); (5) this entry.
- **Gate close (G6):** unit **2445 pass / 2447** (2 WebCrypto Node-18 baseline only) · `npx tsc --noEmit` clean (app **and** `worker/`) · `npm run build:pages` green (`index` < 500 KB; `bundles/1.4.0-rc.zip`). Bump 1.3.5-rc→**1.4.0-rc** (HEADLINE) (package.json + app-version.ts + public/version.json `bundleUrl`→`1.4.0-rc.zip` + RELEASE_NOTES pt/en/es). **Deploy:** commit + push `master` → Cloudflare Pages auto-build. **No `wrangler deploy`** — the peer-ping reuses the already-deployed generic `ShareSignal` DO (the orchestrator anticipated a Worker change, but the relay already exists; the client just addresses a per-actor room). ### G7 — Payments clarity: fairness states + authority + history (done 2026-06-26) → `1.4.1-rc`  [council rec / DEC-353 + DEC-354]
> Julio's pain: *"quem marcou pago aparecia como devedor se o outro não confirmasse"* + *"o organizador confirmava um pagamento feito pra outra pessoa"* + *"quero um histórico de quem pagou quem."* This gate makes group payments **fair + auditable** without touching the arithmetic. **Settlement math UNCHANGED — ledger-math invariance holds** (the new states are metadata over `computeGroupBalances`/`computeGroupTransfers`; the who-paid panel READS the same transfers).
- **m1 · payment lifecycle state machine (`group-payment-status.ts`, pure).** Extended `GroupPaymentStatus` additively: `unpaid → marked(awaiting) → confirmed` + `contested` (receiver rejected a false "paguei") + `cancelled` (mark withdrawn). Data-driven `GROUP_PAYMENT_TRANSITIONS` + `canTransitionGroupPayment` (Â9: every state is revertible). **THE FAIRNESS RULE** = `groupPaymentTone`: `marked` is **`neutral`, NEVER `danger`** — a payer who said "paguei" is not shown red while the receiver hasn't confirmed; only a genuine open debt (`unpaid`) or a rejected claim (`contested`) reads as action-needed. `isGroupObligationClosed` = `confirmed` ONLY (a `marked` payment never counts as settled). `isGroupPaymentActionable` + `groupPaymentStatusLabelKey` for the UI.
- **m2 · append-only activity log (`group-activity.ts` + `GroupActivity` type, pure).** `appendGroupActivity` (immutable, **capped 200** — oldest trimmed so the share payload stays bounded) + `buildGroupActivity` (structured: id/ts/actorId/actorName/kind/subject/counterpart/detail/amount) + `groupActivityTimeline` (newest-first). **Display-only — never a money source**; lives INSIDE the event so it rides the E2E share payload + backup (never Worker-readable — DEC-207).
- **m3 · receiver-confirms + logged override + activity on mutations (F21).** `GroupSplitDetailPage.handleSetPayment` now logs every payment action AND detects authority: when the OWNER confirms a debtor whose creditor (from the transfers) is **not** the owner, it is an **organizer override** → `payment_override` entry ("Júlio confirmou manualmente o pagamento de Ingrid → Débora"); a true receiver-confirm logs `payment_confirmed`. Expense add/remove, share-revoke, and guest joins (folded in the owner poll, null→actorId once) also append entries. `PaymentControl` gained a **contestar** action for a `marked` payment + a `contested` pill.
- **m4 · who-paid / who-falta panel (F22, `buildGroupSettlementStatus`).** Each minimum-transfer obligation tagged with the debtor's lifecycle state + a `settledCount/total` header in the "Quem paga quem" panel — only a `confirmed` debtor counts as settled (a `marked` is still awaiting). A `PaymentTone` pill shows the state in its fairness colour.
- **m5 · history timeline (F23).** A collapsible "Histórico" panel (respects F01 exclusivity via `openPanel`) renders the newest-first timeline (icon + human `t()` string + locale time), capped to 8 with "ver tudo (N)".
- **Compat fixes (correctness):** (a) the **share-payload Zod schema** now accepts `contested`/`cancelled` + the optional `activity[]` — otherwise an event with a member in a new state would parse as a **broken guest link**; (b) the **claim reducer** (`reduceGroupClaims`) preserves owner DECISIONS (confirmed/contested/cancelled) over a guest's self-report, so an owner poll can't silently **undo a contest**.

### G_last — P2 polish: organizer-override audit + per-inviter auto-accept (done 2026-06-26) → `1.4.4-rc`  [DEC-354 + DEC-355]
> The two P2 items trimmed earlier for slack, taken on now that the wave is closed and there's context budget. **Both additive — no ledger/orchestrator math touched.** Item A enriches an existing audit; Item B is a pure client-side convenience over the existing accept-first `group_invite` flow (capability unchanged — still READ-only `/g/` creds).
- **A · richer organizer-override audit (`GroupSplitDetailPage.tsx` + i18n).** When the owner confirms a payment whose creditor is **someone else**, that is an organizer **override** (DEC-354, already logged). `handleSetPayment` now captures `prevStatus` **before** mutating and stores it on the `payment_override` activity's `detail` (display-only token). The timeline renders override rows with an amber **"Ação do organizador"** badge (gavel icon) + the **prior state** it overrode via `overridePrevLabel` → `group_split.override_prev_{unpaid|marked|contested}` (pt/en/es); `override_badge` is the badge label. Pure presentation + one captured field — the activity builder, reducer, and balances are byte-identical.
- **B · per-inviter auto-accept allowlist (`group-link.ts` + `p2p-orchestrators.ts` + `SharedExpensesPage.tsx` + i18n).** A localStorage map (`group.invite.autoaccept`, mirroring `JoinedGroup`) of inviter `actorId`s I trust: `isAutoAcceptInviter` / `addAutoAcceptInviter` / `removeAutoAcceptInviter` / `listAutoAcceptInviters` (empty-actor guard; corrupt blob → `{}`). `InboundP2pItem` now exposes **`fromActorId`** (from the drained inbox row, fallback `envelope.fromActorId`). The `/shared` invite row gained a **"Aceitar convites de {nome} automaticamente"** checkbox (seeded from the persisted list; sets the preference for FUTURE invites only — the on-screen item still needs an explicit Accept, preserving accept-first for what's already shown). `refreshInbox` runs a **silent auto-accept pass**: any pending `group_invite` from a trusted actor is `acceptGroupInvite`+`saveJoinedGroup`'d without a prompt, then the list re-fetches (accept drops the item → no loop). Owner stays the money authority; auto-accept only skips the tap, never grants the write token.
- **Tests (+6 net vs G9):** `group-link.test.ts` auto-accept allowlist suite (opt-in flips `isAutoAcceptInviter`; remove restores the prompt; empty actorId never written; list carries display names; re-trust updates name in place; corrupt blob degrades to `[]`) + a `fromActorId` assertion folded into the existing `p2p-orchestrators` group-invite surface test. **Self-check (5-point):** (1) AC done — override rows flagged + show prior state; a trusted inviter's future invites join silently; the on-screen invite still accept-first; (2) earlier ACs — **ledger-math invariance** (no math edited; A only reads/labels, B folds no money — the `acceptGroupInvite` "folds NO money" test still green), **never-block-contribution** (auto-accept is best-effort; a failed accept just leaves the item pending), **Worker-ciphertext-only** (no Worker diff — the allowlist is local; invites still travel sealed); (3) tests **2497/2499 = +6, 0 new failures** (the 2 are the Node-18 WebCrypto baseline); (4) out-of-scope: none (GroupSplitDetailPage + group-link + p2p-orchestrators + SharedExpensesPage + the 2 test files + i18n + version/release-notes/dev-log); (5) this entry.
- **Gate close (G_last):** unit **2497 pass / 2499** (2 WebCrypto Node-18 baseline only — Node v18.17.0 here; green on Node 22/CI) · `npx tsc --noEmit` clean · `npm run build` green (`index` 418.98 KB < 500 KB; `i18n-locales` 465.55 KB). Bump 1.4.3-rc→**1.4.4-rc** (package.json + app-version.ts + public/version.json `bundleUrl`→`1.4.4-rc.zip` + RELEASE_NOTES pt/en/es). **Deploy:** commit + push `master` → Cloudflare Pages auto-build (**Pages-only OTA — no Worker diff**). Verify `/version.json` + `/bundles/1.4.4-rc.zip`. **DEC-354/355 enrichments → shipped.**

### G9 — Settle-up IA v2 (Variante O) + unified Pessoas + Viagem divisions entry + concept explainers (done 2026-06-26) → `1.4.3-rc`  [✅ L-IA/L-PEOPLE/L-DIV · DEC-356/357/358/359/360]
> The last, largest gate: the ~2240-line `/shared` settle screen stacked ~12 flat near-equal sections, restated money 3–4×, kept two people-lists (Pessoas vs CONEXÕES), and mixed device-backup into the people/settle screen. Recomposed into the **locked Variante O** intent IA. **No ledger/orchestrator logic change — pure re-composition of existing components + one pure view-model.** Ledger-math invariance holds (totals/balances/badges read the same data; nothing was recomputed).
- **m1 · unified people view-model (`domain/connections/people-view.ts`, DEC-357).** A pure `buildPeopleView({participants, peerLinks, ownerId, balances})` merges trip participants + connected peers into ONE `PersonView[]` — one badge vocabulary (`connected · invited · noapp`), `balanceCents`, `needsAction`, `initials` — deduped by `actorId` then folded by name (a manual name that matches a linked person hides behind the real identity; two distinct manual names stay apart; the owner is excluded). `partitionPeople` buckets by status in priority order (need-action → connected → invited → no-app); `searchPeople` is accent/case-insensitive. Zero React/IO. **+12 unit** (`people-view.test.ts` — status derivation, balance, priority sort, dedupe).
- **m2 · recompose `/shared` into Variante O (`SharedExpensesPage.tsx`).** Rather than physically move blocks in a 2240-line file (fragile), every top-level child of the scroll container got an explicit **`order-[N]`** (CSS reorder, DOM untouched — lowest-risk per council). Zones: **(z1) sticky header** (`sticky top-0 z-20 bg-surface`, back · "Acerto de contas" · fixed Meu QR); **(z2) Resolver agora** — a labelled to-do band ABOVE the rest, combining the inbound P2P inbox + pending debts + awaiting-shares, each row keeping its own action; **(z3) Pessoas** — a 3-row rich preview + "ver todas as pessoas (N)" + the connect row WITH the people (Meu QR · Ler QR · Adicionar), never a separate "Conectar" card; **(z4) split explainer**; **(z5) Divisões em grupo pointer** → `/groups` (count + active names, no 2nd list); **(z6) Mais** — Gastos compartilhados collapsed (one tap, not removed), `MirroredStatementsSection`, Histórico/Liquidações, and **Backup MOVED OUT** to a button → `/sync`. The separate "CONEXÕES" people-list is **gone** (connection = a per-person badge now).
- **m3 · full Pessoas view (sanctioned full-screen sheet, DEC-359).** "ver todas as pessoas" opens a full-screen `BottomSheet` (the locked fallback when a new route would duplicate the page's heavy data-loading) with a search box + connect row at top + status sections (`Precisam de ação → Conectados → Convidados → Sem app`), each paginated with "+ ver mais N". Reuses the m1 view-model and the page's already-loaded data + existing handlers — no route, no data duplication.
- **m4 · Viagem-grid divisions entry + FAB→list + rename (`TripHubPage.tsx` + `BottomNav.tsx`, DEC-360).** `/groups` stays the ONE canonical list (no parallel list in `/shared`). The discoverability fix: **(a)** the FAB "Dividir → Divisão em grupo" now lands on **`/groups`** (the list, which has "+ Novo grupo"), not `?new=1` (which hid every existing division behind the create form); **(b)** a new first-level **"Divisões em grupo (N)"** tile in the existing `structureItems` grid → `/groups`, with an active-count badge (loaded via `groupSplitRepository.listEvents(trip.id)`), and the mislabelled "Participantes" tile renamed to **"Acerto de contas"** (`more.settle`, new key). E2E `discovery-clarity-g3` updated: the group door asserts the LIST landed (`/groups` + "Novo grupo" visible), not the auto-opened form.
- **m5 · concept explainers (`components/ConceptHint.tsx`, DEC-358).** ONE reusable collapsible "?" hint — "Qual usar: Dividir conta ou Divisão em grupo?" — contrasting the two modes with concrete examples (restaurante/bar por item × viagem/churrasco em grupo), with a `current` prop that marks "você está aqui". Mounted at the two doors that lacked it: `/split` capture screen (`current="bill"`) and `/groups` (`current="group"`). The FAB chooser + guide/help catalog already carried the contrast (DEC-309/319). i18n `conceptHint.*` pt/en/es.
- **Tests (+14 net vs G8):** the m1 view-model suite (+12) + the people-view dedupe fixes; `discovery-clarity-g3` E2E re-pointed to the list-first group door; the rest of the suite (P2P/settle from G6/G7, group-split math, share-payload) **passes unchanged** (data-invariance). **Self-check (5-point):** (1) AC done — top intent + Resolver in the first viewport; sticky header w/ fixed Meu QR; net money stated once; ONE Pessoas list w/ one badge set; backup NOT on this screen (→ /sync); Gastos + Histórico one tap; every Resolver row keeps a labelled action; the two split modes explained at every door + help; (2) earlier ACs — **ledger-math invariance** (no math edited; the view-model only reads existing balances; group-split + splitting suites green), **never-block-contribution** (no capture path touched), **Worker-ciphertext-only** (no Worker diff — Pages-only OTA); (3) tests **2491/2493 = 0 new failures** (the 2 are the Node-18 WebCrypto baseline); (4) out-of-scope: none (people-view domain + SharedExpensesPage + TripHubPage + BottomNav + SplitPage/GroupSplitListPage mounts + ConceptHint + i18n + version/release-notes/dev-log + the one E2E re-point); (5) this entry.
- **Gate close (G9):** unit **2491 pass / 2493** (2 WebCrypto Node-18 baseline only — Node v18.17.0 here; green on Node 22/CI) · `npx tsc --noEmit` clean · `npm run build` green (`index` 418.98 KB < 500 KB; `i18n-locales` 464.87 KB). Bump 1.4.2-rc→**1.4.3-rc** (package.json + app-version.ts + public/version.json `bundleUrl`→`1.4.3-rc.zip` + RELEASE_NOTES pt/en/es). **Deploy:** commit + push `master` → Cloudflare Pages auto-build (**Pages-only OTA — no Worker diff**; pure UI recomposition). ### G8 — People at creation + the `group_invite` P2P subsystem (done 2026-06-26) → `1.4.2-rc`  [council rec / DEC-355]
> Julio's ask (F24): *"na hora de criar o grupo eu já quero escolher amigos/pessoas da viagem, sem digitar tudo, e o app deles já mostra o grupo."* The mailbox only carried `debt`/`payment`/`connect`, so "a linked friend's app shows the group" needed a **real new P2P kind**, not just a picker. **No money math touched — ledger-math invariance holds** (an invite carries READ creds only; the group folds the same way it always did).
- **m1 · pure people-picker (`domain/group-split/people-picker.ts`).** `buildPeoplePicker({connections, tripParticipants, recentNames})` merges the three sources the app already keeps into ONE de-duplicated, intent-ranked list (connected → trip → recent; same person once). **Dedupe contract:** a trip participant folds into a connection by `linkedActorId` then by name, keeping the **richest identity** (the connection's actorId+key enriched with the trip participantId); a recent name drops once already shown; two different connections sharing a display name stay apart. `filterPeoplePicker` (accent/case-insensitive substring) + `collectRecentGroupNames` (frequency-rank non-owner names across past events). Zero React/IO.
- **m2 · the `group_invite` P2P kind (seal/parse + drain + inbound).** `MailboxPayloadKind` + the envelope `VALID_KINDS` gained **`group_invite`**; new `domain/sync/group-invite-payload.ts` (`{v,shareId,key,groupName}` — the `/g/` **read capability only**, NEVER the writeToken; sealed inside the E2E blob, so the relay never sees it — DEC-207). `sealAndQueue` widened to the kind+payload union; `sendGroupInvite({peerActorId,shareId,key,groupName})` seals to a connected peer (no-op without their key). `drainMailboxIntoApp` parses + enqueues invites PENDING (accept-first ÂNCORA — never auto-joins); `getInboundP2pItems`/`InboundP2pItem` surface them (so the **notification center + home pending card** count them for free); `mailbox-boot` toasts + native-notifies invites (`mailbox.received_invites`).
- **m3 · accept-first UI + materialise on the guest (`SharedExpensesPage` + `group-link.ts`).** The inbox renders a `group_invite` row (groups icon, inviter + group name, Accept/Recusar). `acceptGroupInvite(itemId)` (orchestrator) drops the item + returns the read creds; the page persists a **`JoinedGroup`** (new localStorage registry in `group-link.ts`, guest-side mirror of the owner's `saveGroupLive`) and opens the live `/g/` board — the SAME capability a link grants. Reject just drops it (hide-never-corrupt).
- **m4 · picker wired into the create flow (`GroupSplitListPage`, manual+linked coexist).** A compact, ranked **preview (6) → "ver mais" search** picker above the manual name input; selecting a person toggles a chip (connected dot = green when reachable / amber when re-pair needed). `createGroupSplit` gained `linkedPeople[]` → seeds **`connected`** slots BEFORE manual names, linked by the real trip-participant id **only when that participant is on THIS trip** (a cross-trip link would mis-key the ledger). On create, connected+reachable picks **auto-publish** the group (`publishGroupSplit` + `saveGroupLive`, owner reuses the SAME link) and get a `group_invite` **in parallel**; a typed name that duplicates a picked person is dropped (one slot per person).
- **Tests (+22):** `people-picker.test.ts` (dedupe by actorId/name, richest-identity merge, source ranking, filter, recents ×12); `group-invite-payload.test.ts` (build caps/fallback, **no writeToken**, strict parse fail-closed, envelope round-trip ×6); `p2p-orchestrators.test.ts` (+3 — invite **surfaces** with its creds, **accept** returns creds + clears inbox + **folds NO money**, missing item → null); `create-group-split.test.ts` (+1 — linked people seed `connected` slots, linked-by-id, before manual). **Self-check (5-point):** (1) AC F24 done (pick existing/connected/trip without a giant list; a linked friend's app shows the group **accept-first**; the link uses their real id — invite addressed to actorId, slot linked by participantId); (2) earlier ACs verified — **ledger-math invariance** (accept folds zero money — asserted; all group-split/p2p suites green), **never-block-contribution** (invite send is best-effort; a publish/seal failure still creates the group + shows `created`), **Worker-ciphertext for messages/debts/names/invites** (the invite creds live inside the sealed blob, never as relay metadata — DEC-207; no Worker diff); (3) tests **2477/2479 = +22, 0 new failures**; (4) out-of-scope: none (people-picker domain + group-invite-payload + mailbox type/envelope/sync barrel + p2p/mailbox/group-split orchestrators + mailbox-boot + SharedExpensesPage + GroupSplitListPage + group-link + i18n + version/release-notes/dev-log); (5) this entry.
- **Gate close (G8):** unit **2477 pass / 2479** (2 WebCrypto Node-18 baseline only) · `npx tsc --noEmit` clean (app **and** `worker/`) · `npm run build:pages` green (`index` **418.90 KB** < 500 KB; `bundles/1.4.2-rc.zip` 978 KB). Bump 1.4.1-rc→**1.4.2-rc** (package.json + app-version.ts + public/version.json `bundleUrl`→`1.4.2-rc.zip` + RELEASE_NOTES pt/en/es). **Deploy:** commit + push `master` → Cloudflare Pages auto-build (**Pages-only OTA — no Worker diff**; the `group_invite` kind is opaque to the relay, exactly like `debt`/`payment`/`connect`). Verify `/version.json` + `/bundles/1.4.2-rc.zip`. DEC-355 → APPROVED.

## Grupos, Sincronia & Nuvem wave (2026-06-25) — base 1.1.9-rc → 1.3.0-rc — 🟢 ACTIVE (G0→G7)

> Execution truth: `brain/documents/2026-06-25-groups-sync-cloud-orchestrator.md` (gates G0→G7, change-set **A01–A14/B01/C01–C04/D01–D06**, decisions **DEC-335→347** PROPOSED at G0 → APPROVED per shipping gate; all §16 forks **L1–L8 LOCKED by Julio 2026-06-25**). Three goals: (1) group split feels like a real shared ledger (Expenses-first IA, date+registrant, item-pick capture, everyone contributes), (2) connections become **two-way + live** (mailbox `connect` handshake + drain-on-open/focus + signal-DO peer-ping; live debt/payment with accept-first + notification), (3) shared images get a **cloud home** (R2, **E2E-encrypted**, mirroring the proven FestPilot DEC-059 pattern). **No money-math change in G1–G4 (data-invariance ÂNCORA).** Single continuous session, deploy per gate; G5–G7 also `wrangler deploy`. Most recent gate entry first.

### CURRENT STATE
- **Active gate**: **G7 ✅ shipped `1.3.0-rc` — WAVE COMPLETE (G0→G7)** — **live debt/payment + accept-first + notifications** (DEC-345/346, L7+L8). Once two devices are connected (G6), money flows over the **same E2E mailbox**: **"Cobrar pelo app"** seals a `debt` envelope (my ledger untouched — data-invariance — until they act); the peer drains it to a **PENDING inbox** + a **toast**, and `/shared` shows an **accept-first card** at the top — **Aceitar** folds it as a shared expense (sender = payer, my single **confirmed** share = the amount, auto-resolved onto the active phase's pool via the pure `buildExpenseFromSharedDebt`), **Recusar** drops it. Payments (**"Eu paguei / Eu recebi"**, `announcePaymentToPeer`) close the obligation **on both sides** via a `Settlement` (**never red**); **L8 honored** — whenever I'm the one who RECEIVED the cash, confirmation **always** opens a **fund/wallet picker** and credits it with `createIncomeTransaction` (a real inflow, reusing the funds engine — **not** a duplicate of the expense; the DEC-306 trip bridge stays read-only). Idempotent throughout (`debt:`/`payment:` `externalRef` keys → a redelivery never double-folds). **No Worker change** → Pages-only OTA: the `debt`/`payment` kinds live inside the sealed blob, and live sync already rides **drain-on-open/focus** (the signal-DO **peer-ping D07** is the deferred latency upgrade — DEC-344 already marks the per-pair DO room as V2; correctness/“connect once, then live” holds without it).
- **Last commit**: G7 `1.3.0-rc` `52a33e3` (pushed `master` `36b4a56..52a33e3`) — **Pages OTA verified live**: `/version.json` → `1.3.0-rc` + `/bundles/1.3.0-rc.zip` → `HTTP 200 application/zip`. G6 `1.2.5-rc`; G5 `1.2.4-rc`; G4 `1.2.3-rc`; G3 `1.2.2-rc`; G2 `1.2.1-rc`; G1 `1.2.0-rc`; baseline pre-wave `c37a700` (1.1.9-rc).
- **Tests**: **2388 pass / 2388 collected** (this session's shell runs **Node v22.22.3**, so the 2 WebCrypto `split-live-loop` cases that only fail on Node 18 now **pass** — full green) — **+13 vs G6** (debt/payment payload build/parse/fail-closed + envelope round-trips + ledger-mapper cents + `resolvePaymentParties` ×6; orchestrator accept-folds-expense + idempotency + receiver data-invariance + dismiss + payment-received-credits-fund + payment-paid-no-credit + payment idempotency ×7), **0 new failures, data-invariance held**. `tsc --noEmit` clean (app + Worker) · `build` green (`index` **415.47 KB** < 500 KB). E2E authored per gate, CI-verified. (`npm run lint` is non-functional in this repo — no `eslint.config.js` on disk, eslint v9 needs flat config — a **pre-existing** gap, not introduced here; `tsc -b` is the real gate.)
- **Risks**: without the D07 Worker peer-ping, an inbound debt/payment surfaces on the peer's **next open/focus/online** (drain-on-focus), not sub-second — a latency gap, not a correctness one (toast + accept-first still fire on arrival). The reciprocal **reject-notify** back-channel (so the sender hears a decline) is a small follow-up; today a reject silently drops locally. The send sheets require a **connected peer with a stored `publicKey`** (older pre-mailbox pairings re-share to enable async). `/img` remains an **open** route (E2E by design — ciphertext + per-object cap + TTL guards; ~$1 R2 budget alert).
- **Scope**: G1–G3 UX-only (no backend), G4 owner-as-reducer (Pages-only OTA), G5 R2 images (Worker + Pages), G6 two-way connect + settle IA (Pages-only OTA), G7 live debt/payment (Pages-only OTA — no Worker diff; D07 peer-ping deferred to V2). **Wave done; the only open item is P2 `G_last` (A13 settle-method at creation) + V2 D07 peer-ping.**

Verify `/version.json` + `/bundles/<v>.zip` (+ `/img` after G5).

### G0 — Setup, baseline & brain seed (done 2026-06-25)
- **Env**: Node v18.17.0 / npm 9.6.7 (the EBADENGINE warnings for undici/wrangler are expected in this sandbox; CI runs Node 22). `npm install` up to date. `git` at `/usr/bin/git` (commit bypass confirmed). Project root = `TripPilot/`.

### G1 — Group-split IA + nav loop (done 2026-06-25) → `1.2.0-rc`
- **B01 · DEC-341 · the groups↔detail back loop is gone.** Root cause confirmed: `GroupSplitDetailPage.tsx` in-page back called `navigate('/groups')` (a **push** → a 2nd list entry), while `GroupSplitListPage.tsx:68` back is `navigate(-1)` (a **pop** → back into the detail) = ping-pong. Fix: the detail header back is now `navigate(-1)` (pop to the real parent); the two **structural** navigations — not-found "back to list" and post-delete — use `navigate('/groups', { replace: true })` (replace, never stack a duplicate). The list-page back is unchanged.
- **A03/A04 · DEC-335 · Expenses lead; the math hides behind buttons; "Saldos" is gone.** Reordered the detail: total/people card → **Expenses (primary, first titled section, shown even when empty with its add affordance)** → People → a new **Pagamentos / Quem paga quem** band of toggle buttons (only when `expenses.length > 0`). The balances panel and the transfers panel each render only when their button is tapped (`showBalances`/`showTransfers` state). Renamed the label **"Saldos" → "Pagamentos"** (`balances_title`: pt `Pagamentos`, en `Payments`, es `Pagos`); the transfers button reuses `transfers_title` ("Quem paga quem"). **No math touched** — `computeGroupBalances`/`computeGroupTransfers`/`groupTotalCents` are byte-identical; only render order + visibility + a label string changed (data-invariance ÂNCORA).
- **A02 · DEC-338 (focus part) · adding a person keeps the keyboard.** Added a `newPersonRef` to the detail "People" input; `handleAddPerson` refocuses it after `setNewPerson('')` (the button tap blurs it otherwise). The create-form + `/shared` focus-advance land with their own gates (G3 creation / G6 settle-IA rework).
- **A06 · DEC-339 · invite = link + QR.** When a group is shared (`creds` set), the share card now renders a `QrCodeDisplay` of the `/g/` link + a "scan to join" hint above the copyable link (reuses the existing component; `scan_to_join` added pt/en/es).
- **Tests (+ extended):** new `e2e/groups-sync-g1.spec.ts` (CI) — the back-from-group journey does not loop (lands on `/groups`, a further back leaves the groups area); Expenses heading is present and "Saldos" is absent; add-person keeps the field focused + cleared. Group-split domain suite green (26) — unchanged math. **Self-check:** data-invariance held (balances/transfers/total identical — group-split unit tests pass); expense logging never blocked (the Expenses add affordance only moved up); no Worker touched (ciphertext rule N/A but intact); nothing deleted (Â9 — balances/transfers moved behind buttons, not removed).
- **Gate close (G1):** full unit suite **2336 pass / 2338** (only the 2 WebCrypto `split-live-loop` baseline — **= baseline, 0 new failures**) · `npx tsc --noEmit` clean · `npm run build` green (`index` 410.17 KB < 500 KB). Bump 1.1.9-rc→**1.2.0-rc** (package.json + app-version.ts + public/version.json `bundleUrl`→`1.2.0-rc.zip` + release note pt/en/es). ### G2 — Expense data + capture (done 2026-06-25) → `1.2.1-rc`
- **Domain first (pure, additive — DEC-336/337).** `GroupExpense` gained three optional, non-indexed fields: `occurredAt` (`YYYY-MM-DD` the expense happened), `createdByParticipantId` (who registered it, distinct from the payer), and `items: GroupExpenseLineItem[]` (receipt lines the expense was built from). `buildGroupExpense` sets each only when supplied (legacy rows stay byte-identical → `undefined`). New pure helpers `groupExpenseDayKey` (prefers `occurredAt`, falls back to `createdAt`, slices to a day — no TZ math) and `groupExpensesByDay` (buckets oldest-day-first, preserves in-day order, **exposure only — never touches amounts/balances**). `share-payload.ts` Zod schema extended (`groupExpenseLineItemSchema` + the 3 optionals) so the fields survive the `/g/` link round-trip. All exported via `domain/group-split/index.ts`.
- **A07 · expense date.** The editor has a `Labeled` date input: a **new** expense defaults to today; **editing** a row pre-fills its stored `occurredAt`, else (legacy) its logged day — so editing an old expense never silently jumps it to today. Saved through `buildInput` → `occurredAt`.
- **A11 · registrant.** `buildInput` stamps `createdByParticipantId` = the expense's existing registrant on edit, else the owner participant (owner-authored on this device; guest-authored arrives in G4). The detail row shows a "Registrado por {name}" line **only when the registrant differs from the payer** (no noise when the owner logs their own bill).
- **A12 · day-grouping.** The detail Expenses list renders via `groupExpensesByDay`; a capitalized day header appears **only when the group spans >1 day** (single-day events stay a flat list — no regression). Order matches the old append-order (oldest first).
- **A09 · item-selection capture ("Nota completa | Selecionar itens").** New pure `scanReceiptItemsForGroup(file)` in `group-ai.ts` reuses the same `/ocr` boundary as the whole-bill scan but returns the priced lines. The editor now offers **two scan buttons**: "Nota completa" (the existing summarize→total prefill) and "Selecionar itens" (loads the lines with an `include` toggle each). In item-mode the amount field is replaced by the items list + a live "Total dos itens", and the expense amount **is** the sum of the included lines; a "Usar nota inteira" link exits item-mode. Items are persisted on the expense (`items`).
- **A10 · item view + A08 · bigger fonts.** Opening an expense that has `items` re-opens item-mode showing those lines (A10); the expense row shows a small `checklist` icon when it has items. Fonts bumped to `text-base` for the split-among names + paid-by chips (editor) and the People names + expense titles (detail).
- **i18n (pt/en/es):** added `expense_date`, `scan_full`, `scan_items`, `items_title`, `items_total`, `use_full_bill`, `unnamed_item`, `registered_by`; **removed the now-orphaned `ai_scan`** (replaced by the two scan-mode keys). No string left hard-coded.
- **Tests (+9):** group-split domain +5 (additive-field carry, legacy omission, `groupExpenseDayKey` fallback, day-bucketing order, total-invariance under grouping); group-link +1 (the 3 new fields round-trip the share payload); group-ai +3 (`scanReceiptItemsForGroup` happy path, zero-line→empty, rate-limited). New `e2e/groups-sync-g2.spec.ts` (CI) — date defaults to today + round-trips on edit through a manual expense. **Self-check (5-point):** (1) AC A07/A08/A09/A10/A11/A12 + DEC-336/337 done; (2) earlier ACs verified — **data-invariance** (group-split math tests green; new fields additive, amount in item-mode = Σ included lines, totals/balances untouched), **never-block-expense** (manual entry path unchanged; date defaults so a save is never gated), **Worker-ciphertext-only** (no Worker touched); (3) tests **2345/2347 = +9, 0 new failures**; (4) no out-of-scope files (only group-split domain/feature + its i18n + version/brain); (5) this entry.
- **Gate close (G2):** unit **2345 pass / 2347** (2 WebCrypto baseline only) · `tsc --noEmit` clean · `build` green (`index` **410.32 KB** < 500 KB, +0.16 KB). Bump 1.2.0-rc→**1.2.1-rc** (package.json + app-version.ts + public/version.json `bundleUrl`→`1.2.1-rc.zip` + release note pt/en/es). ### G4 — Everyone contributes (owner-as-reducer) (done 2026-06-25) → `1.2.3-rc`
- **Domain first (pure, additive — DEC-340).** Two additive, non-indexed fields: `GroupExpense.authoredByActorId?` (the guest device that authored it) and `GroupSplitEvent.hiddenExpenseIds?: string[]` (the owner's tombstones). `share-payload.ts` Zod schema carries both over the `/g/` link. **No money-math touched** — `expenseShares`/`computeGroupBalances`/`computeGroupTransfers`/`groupTotalCents` are byte-identical; a guest expense is a *new legitimate input*, not a mutation of existing values (data-invariance ÂNCORA holds: refactors changed nothing; only real new guest inputs add rows).
- **The wire + the reducer (`claim-response.ts`).** `GroupClaimResponse` gained an optional `expenses: GroupClaimExpense[]` — the FULL set a device authors (a snapshot, like `markedPaid`), each with a **client-stable id** (`g:<actorId>:<rand>`). `reduceGroupClaims` now folds them with a strict **add-or-retract, never-replace** contract: ADD a valid, non-tombstoned, unseen authored expense (validated against the event's participants + integer cents → invalid claims dropped, never throw); RETRACT one the author pulled from their snapshot; **never overwrite** an already-folded expense (owner edits win); **skip** any id in `hiddenExpenseIds` (hide-never-delete). `foldClaimExpense` owner-stamps `createdAt`/`source: 'manual'`/`authoredByActorId` and — reusing G2 — the guest's claimed slot as `createdByParticipantId` ("Registrado por …"). `removeExpense` auto-tombstones a guest-authored row so a stale snapshot can't resurrect it.
- **A05 · the guest can author (GroupClaimPage).** Once a `/g/` guest picks their name, an inline **"Adicionar despesa"** form (description + amount + who-paid chips, split equal across everyone) appends to a per-share local draft (`group.guest.expenses`, re-validated through the domain schema on load → a corrupt draft never posts). The draft rides the **same** debounced claim post (one channel for paid-flag + expenses). The board merges the owner's ledger with my draft: my un-folded rows show **"pendente"**, my folded rows are removable (→ retract), and rows the owner **tombstoned** are pruned from my draft on the next poll (honest sync — no ghosts). Item view: a guest expense with receipt lines shows them inline.
- **Owner fold wiring (GroupSplitDetailPage).** The owner poll already folded paid-claims; it now persists/re-publishes when **participants OR expenses** change (the prior check only compared participants — would have silently dropped folded expenses). Still deterministic + idempotent (only a real diff writes → converges, never loops).
- **A14 · "baixe o app" CTA.** A gentle web-guest nudge linking to the public origin (the PWA), never store-gated. i18n (pt/en/es): added `group_claim.add_expense/add_expense_hint/expense_desc_ph/who_paid/pending/your_tag/remove/get_app_title/get_app_cta`. No string hard-coded.
- **Tests (+6):** `group-link.test.ts` "guest-authored expenses (DEC-340)" — folds + stamps author + **balances cents-exact** (A paid 9000/share 4000 = +5000; B = −1000; C = −4000; Σ=0); idempotent re-fold (no dup, first stamp kept); author retract; never-overwrite (tampered amount ignored); owner-tombstone blocks resurrection; invalid claims (unknown payer/share) dropped. New `e2e/groups-sync-g4.spec.ts` (CI) — real two-context: a `/g/` guest authors "Taxi G4", it shows pending, folds into the owner's board, and returns to the guest no-longer-pending. **Self-check (5-point):** (1) AC A05 + A14 + DEC-340 done; (2) earlier ACs verified — **data-invariance** (no math edited; guest expenses are new inputs; fold tests assert exact cents), **never-block-expense** (the guest add path is additive; a blank/zero is the only thing that disables Add, the owner path is untouched), **Worker-ciphertext-only** (no Worker touched — Pages-only OTA; the share channel still posts opaque ciphertext); (3) tests **2354/2356 = +6, 0 new failures**; (4) no out-of-scope files (group-split domain/feature + GroupClaimPage + group-link + detail-page fold check + i18n + version/brain); (5) this entry.
- **Gate close (G4):** unit **2354 pass / 2356** (2 WebCrypto baseline only) · `tsc --noEmit` clean · `build` green (`index` **411.99 KB** < 500 KB). Bump 1.2.2-rc→**1.2.3-rc** (package.json + app-version.ts + public/version.json `bundleUrl`→`1.2.3-rc.zip` + release note pt/en/es). Brain: **DEC-340 → APPROVED**. **Deploy:** commit + push `master` → Cloudflare Pages auto-build (Pages-only; G5 starts the Worker work).

### G5 — R2 E2E-encrypted images (done 2026-06-25) → `1.2.4-rc`
- **Worker first (R2 channel — DEC-342/343, mirrors FestPilot DEC-059 but private).** `worker/wrangler.jsonc` gained an `[[r2_buckets]]` binding `MEDIA → trippilot-media`; `worker/src/index.ts` added `handleImg` routing `/img/:id`: **PUT** stores the body as `application/octet-stream` (id validated `^[0-9a-fA-F-]{8,64}$`, empty→400, >2.1 MB→413, `Cache-Control: immutable`, `customMetadata.expiresAt` from a clamped `X-Img-TTL`); **GET** streams the ciphertext but first runs the **anonymous TTL guard** (expired → delete-on-read → 404); **DELETE** drops it. The Worker **never sees a key** — secrecy is the per-image AES key in the E2E payload (ÂNCORA: Worker stores only ciphertext). Absent binding → `images_not_configured` (older deploys keep photos device-local).
- **Crypto (binary E2E).** `data/sync/crypto.ts` added `encryptBytes`/`decryptBytes` — the same AES-GCM + 12-byte IV-prefix scheme as the text path but raw-bytes in/out (no base64 bloat on the wire); `encryptBytes` returns a concretely `ArrayBuffer`-backed `Uint8Array` so it's a clean `BlobPart` for the upload. Fails closed (null) on wrong key / tampered bytes.
- **Domain (pure — `domain/media/`).** New `ImageRef { r2Id, key, mime, w, h }` (the only thing that travels, inside an already-E2E payload), `IMAGE_MAX_PLAINTEXT_BYTES = 2_000_000` / `IMAGE_MAX_CIPHERTEXT_BYTES = 2_100_000`, a pure `checkImageBytes` cap verdict (empty / too_large / ok mirroring the Worker), and `imageRefSchema` (Zod). `GroupExpense.imageRef?` is additive + non-indexed; `share-payload.ts` **and** `claim-response.ts` carry it (future-proofs guest photo authoring — `foldClaimExpense` copies a claimed `imageRef`). **No money-math touched** (data-invariance ÂNCORA).
- **Boundary (`data/sync/media-link.ts`).** The only place wiring `ImageRef` to network + WebCrypto: `uploadEncryptedImage` (fresh **per-image** key → encrypt → PUT, plaintext cap then ciphertext-ceiling re-check), `fetchDecryptedImageUrl` (GET → decrypt → object URL, null on gone/expired/bad-key so a broken image never throws into render), `deleteSharedImage` (best-effort DELETE; the TTL reaps any orphan).
- **UI — owner (`GroupExpenseEditor` + `GroupSplitDetailPage` + `GroupImage`).** The editor gained a **Fotos** block (attach via the existing `ImageSourceChooser`, compress, cap-check, preview; replace supersedes the old, remove clears) and hands back a `GroupExpenseImageIntent { pending, removeExisting }` on save — it **never touches transport** (the detail page owns the share creds). The detail page reconciles: a **reconcile-on-share** effect encrypts+uploads any pending photo once the event is shared, folds the `ImageRef` in, re-publishes (guarded/idempotent — never double-uploads or races the claim poll); a stale-refs queue flushes deletions (remove/replace/revoke). New **Fotos gallery** (uploaded refs via `GroupImage` decrypt-on-mount; a pending photo shows the local thumb with a `cloud_upload` badge) + a photo icon on rows + a download **lightbox** (revokes only the blobs it owns). **delete-on-revoke**: `handleRevoke` deletes every uploaded blob and strips the refs locally so nothing renders broken.
- **UI — guest (`GroupClaimPage`).** A folded expense with an `imageRef` shows a `GroupImage` thumbnail on its row and opens the same lightbox (the per-image key rode the share payload, so the no-app guest decrypts it client-side too). The blob URL is owned/revoked by the `GroupImage` that produced it — the board lightbox only clears its reference.
- **i18n (pt/en/es):** `add_photo`, `remove_photo`, `photo_too_large`, `photo_pending_hint`, `photos_title`, `image_download`. No string hard-coded.
- **Tests (+11):** `domain/media/image-ref.test.ts` (+8) — cap accepts ≤2 MB, rejects empty/zero/NaN as `empty` and oversize as `too_large`, re-checks the explicit ciphertext ceiling, ceiling > plaintext cap; `imageRefSchema` parses a valid ref + rejects a missing key / non-numeric dims. `data/sync-crypto.test.ts` (+3) — `encryptBytes`/`decryptBytes` round-trip **byte-for-byte** (length = plaintext + 12 IV + 16 tag), unique IV per image (same bytes → different ciphertext), fail-closed on wrong key / single-bit tamper. **Self-check (5-point):** (1) AC C01–C04 + DEC-342/343 done; (2) earlier ACs verified — **data-invariance** (no money math edited; `imageRef` is additive metadata — totals/balances byte-identical, group-split suite green), **never-block-expense** (the photo block is optional + a failed/oversized upload toasts and keeps pending — saving an expense is never gated on a photo), **Worker-ciphertext-only** (Worker stores `application/octet-stream`, key never leaves the device); (3) tests **2367/2367 = +11, 0 new failures** (Node 22 here → the 2 WebCrypto baseline cases also pass); (4) no out-of-scope files (media domain + boundary + crypto + group-split editor/detail/claim + GroupImage + Worker `/img` + i18n + version/brain); (5) this entry.
- **Gate close (G5):** unit **2367 pass / 2367** (Node v22.22.3 in-session — full green) · `tsc --noEmit` clean (app **and** `worker/`) · `build` green (`index` **412.68 KB** < 500 KB). Bucket `trippilot-media` created + Worker **deployed** (`wrangler deploy`); live **`/img` PUT 200 → GET 200 octet-stream exact-bytes → DELETE 200 → GET 404** smoke-verified. Bump 1.2.3-rc→**1.2.4-rc** (package.json + app-version.ts + public/version.json `bundleUrl`→`1.2.4-rc.zip` + release note pt/en/es). Brain: **DEC-342 + DEC-343 → APPROVED**. **Deploy:** commit + push `master` → Cloudflare Pages auto-build **+ `wrangler deploy`** (Worker R2 binding + `/img` routes).

### G6 — Two-way connect handshake + settle-up IA (done 2026-06-25) → `1.2.5-rc`
- **Domain first (pure — DEC-344).** New `domain/sync/connect-payload.ts`: `ConnectPayload { actorId(uuid), name(≤60), pk }` + `buildConnectPayload` (trim/cap, mirrors the envelope name cap) + `parseConnectPayload` (Zod `safeParse` → null, **fails closed** on bad uuid / empty name / missing key / garbage). `MailboxPayloadKind` gained **`'connect'`** and `mailbox-envelope.ts`'s `VALID_KINDS` accepts it — the transport is **kind-agnostic**, so the `connect` kind lives **inside the sealed blob** and the Worker still forwards only opaque ciphertext (**ÂNCORA: Worker stores only ciphertext** — zero Worker change).
- **Receive side (`sync-orchestrators.ts`).** `upsertPeerLinkFromConnect(connect)` wraps the existing private `upsertPeerLink(actorId, name, **null**, pk)` — a drained `connect` upserts the **reverse `peerLink`** with the peer's public key. `participantId: null` ⇒ the upsert **preserves any existing trip mapping** (never wipes a ledger link) and creates **no participant** (the peer surfaces under "Conexões", added to a trip in one tap when wanted) — **ledger-neutral** (data-invariance ÂNCORA; same guarantee the reconnect path is tested for).
- **Send side + composition (`mailbox-orchestrators.ts`).** `sendConnectHandshake({ actorId, publicKey, name? })` — after I pair (so I hold the peer's pk), seal a `connect` envelope carrying **my** identity (device actorId + name + my public key) and queue+flush it to the peer's mailbox; no-op if the peer has no key (paired pre-mailbox). To wire all call sites **without a sync→mailbox import cycle**, two composed orchestrators live here (mailbox already imports sync): `connectPeerFromIdentity(identity, tripId)` = `pairParticipantFromIdentity` **+ best-effort** handshake; `linkConnectFromIdentity(participantId, identity, tripId)` = `linkParticipantToIdentity` **+ best-effort** handshake. Best-effort = the local pairing has already succeeded; an offline peer just gets the reverse link on a later re-pair/drain (**honest** — pairing never throws on a transient send).
- **Drain routing + boot (`drainMailboxIntoApp` + `mailbox-boot.ts`).** `DrainResult` gained `connects`; a drained `connect` → `parseConnectPayload` → `upsertPeerLinkFromConnect` (invalid skipped). `mailbox-boot` surfaces `connects > 0` with a toast (`mailbox.received_connections`) and the existing `MAILBOX_DRAINED_EVENT` so `/shared` refreshes — **honest sync state** (counts only links actually folded; `delivered` reflects the real flush, no fake "delivered").
- **Call sites (D01/D02).** `PairPage` (link-open confirm) → `connectPeerFromIdentity`; `SharedExpensesPage` all three pair entry points (QR scan-to-add, retroactive link-by-QR, re-add a known friend) → `connectPeerFromIdentity` / `linkConnectFromIdentity`. So **every** way you add someone now also announces you back.
- **Settle-up IA (D05/D06 · DEC-347 — `SharedExpensesPage`).** **"Meu QR" pinned top-right** in the header (always reachable — someone can add me any time, instead of it hiding at the bottom of Conexões). The "how splitting works" explainer **moved below People** so the add-person row sits higher. **Conexões open by default** (still kept MOUNTED when collapsed so the live mirror sockets keep running). The old in-section duplicate "Meu QR" was removed (now in the header) and the `/sync` device-transfer was **relabelled + fenced as "Backup de aparelho"** (own-device data move) with a `devices` icon — visually separated from connecting a friend, killing the QR asymmetry that read as a second add-person path.
- **i18n (pt/en/es):** `mailbox.received_connections`, `shared.device_backup_label` / `device_backup_title` / `device_backup_desc`, and a rewritten `shared.connections_hint` (drops the now-top-right "show your QR"). No string hard-coded.
- **Tests (+8):** `domain/sync/connect-payload.test.ts` (+5) — build carries identity+pk, trims/caps the name, parse accepts a well-formed payload + **fails closed** on non-uuid / empty name / missing key / null / garbage, and the **connect envelope round-trips** pack→unpack preserving the payload. `domain/orchestrators/connect-handshake.test.ts` (+3, fake-indexeddb) — `upsertPeerLinkFromConnect` creates a fresh reverse link (name+pk, `participantId` null, `lastSyncAt` set); **updates an existing link without wiping its participant mapping** (ledger-neutral) and refreshes name+key, exactly one link (upsert not duplicate); refreshes `lastSyncAt` (honest "last seen"). **Self-check (5-point):** (1) AC D01/D02/D05/D06 + DEC-344/347 done; (2) earlier ACs verified — **data-invariance** (no money math touched; the handshake only writes `peerLink` delivery addresses, `participantId: null` preserves mappings — group-split + sync suites green), **never-block-expense** (no expense path touched; pairing is best-effort so a transient send never blocks), **Worker-ciphertext-only** (zero Worker change — `connect` rides the sealed mailbox blob); (3) tests **2375/2375 = +8, 0 new failures** (Node 22 → the 2 WebCrypto baseline cases also pass); (4) no out-of-scope files (connect-payload domain + mailbox/sync orchestrators + barrel + 2 features + mailbox-boot + i18n + 2 tests + version/brain); (5) this entry.
- **Gate close (G6):** unit **2375 pass / 2375** (Node v22.22.3 in-session — full green) · `tsc --noEmit` clean · `build` green (`index` **413.86 KB** < 500 KB, +1.18 KB). Bump 1.2.4-rc→**1.2.5-rc** (package.json + app-version.ts + public/version.json `bundleUrl`→`1.2.5-rc.zip` + release note pt/en/es). ### G7 — Live debt/payment + accept-first + notifications (done 2026-06-25) → `1.3.0-rc`
- **Domain first (pure, additive — DEC-345/346).** Two new `MailboxPayloadKind`s, `debt` + `payment` (added to `mailbox.ts` + `mailbox-envelope.ts` `VALID_KINDS`; opaque to the Worker — they live inside the sealed blob). New pure Zod payloads: `SharedDebtPayload` (`debt-payload.ts` — `debtId` UUID, sender, currency, `amountCents`, `description`, `occurredAt`) and `PaymentPayload` (`payment-payload.ts` — `paymentId` UUID, `direction: 'paid' | 'received'`, `amountCents`, `note`), each with `build*`/`parse*` (fail-closed). New `inbound-ledger.ts` ledger mappers (PURE, **reuse the canonical engines — no new money math**): `buildExpenseFromSharedDebt` (→ `createExpenseTransaction` with the sender as `paidByParticipantId` + walletId null per the DEC-114 "other paid" truth-table + a single **confirmed** `createCustomShares` slot = my owed amount + `externalRef` idempotency key `debt:<actorId>:<debtId>`), `externalRefForDebt`, and `resolvePaymentParties` (direction→debtor/creditor + `iReceived`, the L8 driver). All exported via `domain/sync/index.ts`.
- **Orchestrators (`p2p-orchestrators.ts`, DB layer — m3/m4).** Send: `shareDebtWithPeer` (seal a `debt` → `enqueueOut` → `flushOutbox`; **my ledger is untouched** — data-invariance — the obligation is born on the recipient when THEY accept) and `announcePaymentToPeer` (settles MY side immediately via `createSettlement` — the cash really moved — and when I RECEIVED it credits my chosen fund via `createIncomeTransaction`, then seals a `payment` to the peer). Inbox actions: `acceptInboundDebt` (resolve-or-create the sender participant by `linkedActorId` → fold via `buildExpenseFromSharedDebt` onto the caller-resolved active phase/pool → `transactionRepository.create` + `participantShareRepository.bulkCreate`; **idempotent** — skips if the `externalRef` already exists), `confirmInboundPayment` (close both sides via a `Settlement` with `externalRef` `payment:<actorId>:<paymentId>`; **L8** — when `iReceived`, also `createIncomeTransaction` into the chosen fund/wallet; idempotent on the settlement ref), `getInboundP2pItems` (parse the PENDING inbox for the UI), `dismissInboundP2p` (drop). Barrel-exported. `drainMailboxIntoApp` extended with `debt`/`payment` branches → `enqueueIn` (PENDING, **never auto-folded** — accept-first ÂNCORA) + `DrainResult.debts`/`payments` counts.
- **Notifications.** `mailbox-boot.ts` `runMailboxSync` surfaces the new counts as toasts (`mailbox.received_debts`/`received_payments`) and fires `MAILBOX_DRAINED_EVENT` so an open `/shared` refreshes its inbox without a navigation. Live arrival rides the existing **drain-on-open/focus/online** (the boot sync). The signal-DO **peer-ping (D07)** — the sub-second push — is **deferred to V2** (DEC-344 already marks the per-pair DO room as V2): it's a latency upgrade on top of an already-live sync, not a correctness gap, so G7 ships with **zero Worker diff**.
- **UI (`SharedExpensesPage`, m6).** An **accept-first inbox** section pinned at the top: a debt card (`{name} sent you a charge` + description + amount) with **Aceitar/Recusar**, and a payment card (`{name} says they paid you` / `… confirmed you paid`) with **Confirmar/Dispensar**; busy-guarded, refreshed on `MAILBOX_DRAINED_EVENT`. Accept auto-resolves the active phase + its operational pool (`resolveActivePhase` + `selectActivePhasePool` — the same resolvers the dashboard/receipt use) so it is genuinely one tap. **L8 fund picker** (`FundPicker` — pool chips + wallet chips + "don't track in a wallet"): shown when confirming a payment I received and in the "Eu recebi" send path, defaulting to the active phase's pool. Send entry points on a connected peer's statement sheet: **"Cobrar pelo app"** (amount + what-for) and **"Registrar pagamento"** (Eu paguei / Eu recebi + amount + fund picker when received). Three new `BottomSheet`s (charge / pay / confirm-with-fund).
- **i18n (pt/en/es):** `mailbox.received_debts`/`received_payments` + a new **`p2p`** namespace (inbox labels, accept/reject/confirm/dismiss, fund picker, charge/pay sheets, queued/no-key states). No string hard-coded.
- **Tests (+13):** `domain/sync/p2p-ledger.test.ts` (+6) — debt/payment build·parse·fail-closed + envelope round-trips; `buildExpenseFromSharedDebt`×`calculateDebts` cents-exact; `resolvePaymentParties` both directions. `domain/orchestrators/p2p-orchestrators.test.ts` (+7, real fake-indexeddb, network-free) — **receiver data-invariance** (a queued debt changes no totals until accept), accept folds an exact-cents shared expense (sender=payer, confirmed share, `calculateDebts` reads "I owe sender X"), accept idempotency (redelivery never double-folds), dismiss is ledger-neutral, **L8** payment-I-received settles toward me **and** credits the chosen pool (income tx cents-exact), payment-I-paid settles away with **no** income, payment idempotency. **Self-check (5-point):** (1) AC D03/D04 + DEC-345/346 (L8) done; D07 peer-ping deferred to V2 (documented). (2) earlier ACs verified — **data-invariance** (inbound items sit PENDING; only an explicit accept/confirm writes; mappers reuse the existing engines; cents asserted), **never-block-expense** (P2P is additive; the local expense/income paths are untouched), **Worker-ciphertext-only** (no Worker touched — the new kinds are opaque, sealed inside the blob). (3) tests **2388/2388 = +13, 0 new failures**. (4) no out-of-scope files (sync domain payloads/mapper + p2p orchestrator + drain/boot + `SharedExpensesPage` + i18n + version/brain). (5) this entry.
- **Gate close (G7):** unit **2388 pass / 2388** (Node v22.22.3 in-session — full green) · `tsc --noEmit` clean · `build` green (`index` **415.47 KB** < 500 KB, +1.61 KB). Bump 1.2.5-rc→**1.3.0-rc** (headline — live two-way money) (package.json + app-version.ts + public/version.json `bundleUrl`→`1.3.0-rc.zip` + release note pt/en/es). Brain: **DEC-345 + DEC-346 → APPROVED**. **No `wrangler deploy`** — G7 has **zero Worker diff** (D07 peer-ping deferred to V2; the `debt`/`payment` kinds are opaque to the Worker), so the live Worker already matches committed source from G5. **Deploy:** commit + push `master` → Cloudflare Pages auto-build (Pages-only OTA). **Wave G0→G7 COMPLETE.**

Deepest root: `routePlannedExpense` maps a dated new-pot to `event_new_pot` (Event + dated global pot) — the E01 bug; `getAvailablePoolsForPhase` omits off-phase `linked_phases` pools — E02; `/quick-add` & `/income` render outside `AppShell` so `openAssistant()` has no subscriber — E03.
