### G1 — Scrollbar lock (E11) · global assistant (E03) · labelled import (E04) (done 2026-06-25)
- **E11 · DEC-331 · the side scrollbar is locked for good.** Root cause confirmed: `globals.css` already hid scrollbars via `*::-webkit-scrollbar{display:none}` + `scrollbar-width:none` on `html,body,*`, **but the document/viewport scrollbar is a special pseudo of the root that `*` does NOT reliably match across Chrome / Android WebView versions** — so the root bar leaked back "on every screen". Fix: target the root explicitly — `html::-webkit-scrollbar, body::-webkit-scrollbar, *::-webkit-scrollbar { width:0; height:0; display:none !important }` — and added `!important` to the universal `scrollbar-width: none` / `-ms-overflow-style: none` so no later utility/inline rule can re-expose a bar. **Trava (test):** `style-hygiene.test.ts` C03 now asserts (a) the `!important` universal hide, (b) the explicit `html/body::-webkit-scrollbar` rule, and (c) bans `overflow*: scroll` + Tailwind `overflow-scroll` utilities (the always-on-track regression vector) in addition to the existing gutter/auto/thin/color ban. No source file currently violates it. `globals.css` loads AFTER `@tailwind utilities` (plain CSS, no `@layer`), so it wins the cascade.
- **E03 · DEC-323 · the AI button is no longer a dead end.** Root cause: `<AssistantSheet />` was mounted only inside `AppShell`, but `/quick-add` and `/income` are **siblings** of `AppShell` under `RootLayout` — so `openAssistant()` published to a bus with no subscriber and the purple button did nothing. Fix: mounted `<AssistantSheet />` once at **`RootLayout`** (inside `AppDataProvider`, behind `AppLockGate`), removed it from `AppShell`, and added a defensive `if (!trip) return null` after all hooks in `AssistantSheet` so guest boards (`/s`, `/t`, `/g`) / pre-onboarding routes never mount it. Now the assistant opens from every route (Quick Add, Income, the pure tools).
- **E04 · DEC-324 · the import entry is discoverable.** `ExpenseListPage` statement-import button went from icon-only (`upload_file`, aria-label only) to a labelled chip mirroring the "Escanear" chip: `upload_file` + visible **"Importar"** (`expenses.import_statement_short`, new pt/en/es), full label kept in aria-label/title. Neutral `bg-surface-container` (not the AI accent — import isn't AI), so the M12 hex-token test stays green.
- **Tests (+ extended):** `style-hygiene.test.ts` 14→16 unit tests (C03 hardened, +1 root-scrollbar `it`). New `e2e/field-fixes-2-g1.spec.ts` (CI): AI button opens the assistant from `/quick-add`; the expenses import button shows its label. **Self-check:** no domain math touched (Â11 — cofrinho/insight/budget identical to baseline); expense logging path untouched; nothing deleted (Â9 — the assistant just moved up the tree, the import button only gained a label).
- **Gate close (G1):** full unit suite **2315 collected, 2313 pass** (only the 2 WebCrypto `split-live-loop` baseline; +1 vs G0) · `npx tsc --noEmit` clean · `npm run build` green (`index` 409.31 KB < 500 KB). Bump 1.1.3-rc→**1.1.4-rc** (package.json + app-version.ts + public/version.json `bundleUrl`→`1.1.4-rc.zip` + release note pt/en/es). Brain: DEC-323 + DEC-324 + DEC-331 → APPROVED. **Deploy:** commit + push `master` → Cloudflare Pages auto-build.

### G2 — Off-phase funds selectable in expense + income (E02) (done 2026-06-25)
- **E02 · DEC-322 · a fund from another phase is now selectable when logging.** Root cause: `getAvailablePoolsForPhase` (`domain/budget/budget.ts`) returned only `{operational, global}` where `operational` = `linked_phases` pools linked to the **active** phase; a `linked_phases` pool linked to **another** phase was in neither bucket, so neither `QuickAddPage` nor `IncomePage` (`selectablePools = [...operational, ...global]`) ever listed it — you could create the Eurotrip fund (`/funds → vinculado a fases`) but not spend/earn against it from the Burgos phase. **Fix (domain-first, no math change · Â11):** the `AvailablePools` interface gains `otherPhases: BudgetPool[]`; `getAvailablePoolsForPhase` now computes the `linked_phases` pools linked to a **non-active** phase (excluding any already in `operational`, so legacy multi-phase pools never double-list) and returns them as a third group. `autoSelectedPoolId` is unchanged (still only a lone `operational` pool — off-phase funds are **never** auto-selected).
- **Both pickers (E02).** `QuickAddPage.tsx` and `IncomePage.tsx`: the `availablePools` fallback gains `otherPhases: []`; `selectablePools` now appends `availablePools.otherPhases`; a new labelled section **"Fundos de outras fases"** (i18n `expenses.fund_other_phases`, `schedule` icon) renders those pools below the current-phase + global groups. In Income, `effectivePoolId` explicitly excludes `otherPhases` from any auto-pick — selectable, never default. ÂNCORA 9 — nothing removed; the off-phase fund only **gains** reachability.
- **Tests (+ extended):** `domain/budget/budget.test.ts` +4 — off-phase `linked_phases` pool is exposed as `otherPhases`, is **not** in `operational`, is **not** auto-selected (`autoSelectedPoolId` stays the active-phase pool / null), and a legacy pool linked to *both* the active and another phase is **not** duplicated into `otherPhases`. New `e2e/field-fixes-2-g2.spec.ts` (CI): create a future-phase fund via `/funds`, then confirm it appears + is selectable under "Fundos de outras fases" on both `/quick-add` and `/income`. **Self-check:** cofrinho/insight/budget math untouched (Â11 — the selector only *widens what is listable*, it does not change any total, the active-phase budget signal still reads only `operational`); expense logging never blocked (the new group only adds options); nothing deleted (Â9).
- **Gate close (G2):** full unit suite **2318 collected, 2316 pass** (only the 2 WebCrypto `split-live-loop` baseline; +3 vs G1) · `npx tsc --noEmit` clean · `npm run build` green (`index` 409.46 KB < 500 KB) · **scrollbar lock intact** (style-hygiene test green). Bump 1.1.4-rc→**1.1.5-rc** (package.json + app-version.ts + public/version.json `bundleUrl`→`1.1.5-rc.zip` + release note pt/en/es). Brain: DEC-322 → APPROVED. **Deploy:** commit + push `master` → Cloudflare Pages auto-build.

### G3 — Create door reframed: Pote/Fundo is phase-scoped, never an Event (E01) (done 2026-06-25)
- **E01 · DEC-321 · the door's first question is now the INTENT, so a phase fund is never misfiled as a dated Event.** Root cause: the door opened with **"Acontece numa data?"**; choosing *sim* + *valor à parte (novo pote)* routed `routePlannedExpense(hasDate=true, 'new_pot')` → `event_new_pot`, which created a **countdown Event + a dated GLOBAL pot** — so "Hospedagem Eurotrip 15/07–04/08" polluted the Burgos Home with a phantom event, and the global pot wasn't the phase fund the user wanted (and wasn't selectable from the Burgos phase). The user's real intent ("guardar dinheiro para a próxima fase") is the working `/funds → vinculado a fases` path. **Fix (domain-first, no math change · Â11):**
  - **`domain/planning/plan-routing.ts`** — new `PotScope = 'trip' | 'phase'` and a new outcome **`pot_phase`**; `routePlannedExpense(hasDate, funding, potScope='trip')` routes an undated new-pot to `pot_phase` when `potScope==='phase'` (else `pot`, the global pot — unchanged). New classifier `outcomeCreatesPhasePot`; `outcomeCreatesNewPot` now also covers `pot_phase`. Dated branches are **byte-identical** (Event behavior untouched). Exported via `domain/planning/index.ts`.
  - **`domain/orchestrators/plan-orchestrators.ts`** — `createPlannedExpense` takes optional `potScope`; for `pot_phase` it creates a **`linked_phases` BudgetPool tied to the chosen `phaseId`** via `createBudgetPoolWithPhaseLinks` (the exact `/funds` path) — **no `PlannedExpense`, no `PlannedOccurrence`, no countdown** — and an optional savings goal is honored (a fund is something you save toward). The active trecho is left untouched. `pot` (trip-wide) still makes a global pot exactly as before.
- **The door (`features/trip/PlanExpenseSheet.tsx`).** Q0 is now **"O que você quer criar?"** → **um gasto/evento** (the old date-fork: *com data* → Event + countdown · *sem data* → Compra) × **um pote/fundo** (no date, no countdown). Picking *pote/fundo* asks **"Para qual parte da viagem?"** → *uma fase específica* (→ `pot_phase`, a phase-scoped fund off the current Home, selectable when logging — E02) · *a viagem toda* (→ `pot`, a global pot). `new_pot` funding is no longer offered for an **undated** spend (setting money aside IS the fund intent — removes the old accidental dated-global-pot path). Name placeholder/example chips/validation adapt to the intent; a savings goal shows only for a fund. No fund/pool/occurrence jargon shows.
- **i18n.** `plan.kind_q/kind_spend(_desc/_note)/kind_fund(_desc/_note)`, `plan.fund_scope_q/_phase(_desc)/_trip(_desc)`, `plan.fund_phase_label`, `plan.no_phase_fund`, `plan.name_placeholder_fund`, `plan.created_pot_phase`; reworded `plan.q1_no(_desc)` to "a planned purchase — no date" (the fund left this branch). pt-BR/en/es.
- **Tests (+ extended):** `plan-routing.test.ts` — `pot_phase` routing for both `potScope` values, undated default still `pot`, and the new classifiers. `plan-orchestrators.test.ts` +5 — a phase fund creates a `linked_phases` pool + phase link, **no occurrence/date**, leaves the active trecho untouched, honors an optional goal, requires a `phaseId`, and the created pool is surfaced by `getAvailablePoolsForPhase` as `otherPhases` from another phase (the E02 cross-check). E2E (CI): rewrote `discovery-clarity-g5.spec.ts` test 1 for the intent-first door; new `e2e/field-fixes-2-g3.spec.ts` (plan door → pote/fundo → fase Madrid → created; absent from the Burgos Home focus; selectable under "Fundos de outras fases" on `/quick-add`). **Self-check:** cofrinho/insight/budget math untouched (Â11 — only *what is created* changed, no total recomputed); the dated-Event path is unchanged (existing event/event_new_pot tests green); expense logging never blocked; nothing deleted (Â9 — the date question moved under "um gasto/evento"; the global-pot path stays as the "viagem toda" scope).
- **Gate close (G3):** full unit suite **2325 collected, 2323 pass** (only the 2 WebCrypto `split-live-loop` baseline; +7 vs G2) · `npx tsc --noEmit` clean · `npm run build` green (`index` 409.46 KB < 500 KB) · scrollbar lock intact. Bump 1.1.5-rc→**1.1.6-rc** (package.json + app-version.ts + public/version.json `bundleUrl`→`1.1.6-rc.zip` + release note pt/en/es). Brain: DEC-321 → APPROVED. **Deploy:** commit + push `master` → Cloudflare Pages auto-build.

### G4 — Home clarity: compact check-in (E06) · cofrinho-covered (E08) · first-run Descobrir (E05) (done 2026-06-25)
- **E06 · DEC-326 · the day check-in moved onto the hero's "Posso gastar" row.** The standalone `daily_checkin` card is **retired from the movable catalog** (`domain/dashboard/dashboard-cards.ts` — removed from `DashboardCardId` + `DASHBOARD_CARD_CATALOG`; legacy saved orders containing it are dropped gracefully by `resolveDashboardCardSequence`, like any unknown id). In `DashboardCards.tsx` the check-in body (picker + the chosen mode's read-only payoff) became a `renderCheckIn()` helper rendered **inside the hero**, opened from a compact chip that sits in the space the shrunken "Posso gastar" (DEC-316) left on its row. State: `isCheckInExpanded = !effectiveCheckInIntent || checkInExpanded` — with no intention the picker is open (nothing to summarise yet); choosing one collapses the chip to a one-line "Intenção de hoje: …" that re-expands on tap (accordion). The check-in **lens** that spotlights a card below now anchors after **`hero`** (was `daily_checkin`). **No math change (Â11)** — the result block, `resolveSavingDestination`, and the single saving-destination rule are byte-identical, only relocated.
- **E08 · DEC-328 · a negative day reads honestly.** New read-only block under the hero's free-today number (`DashboardCards.tsx`, `data-piggy-covered`): when `baseFreeTodayCents < 0` **and** the piggy ledger withdrew today (`todayEntry.deltaCents < 0`), it states the cofrinho covered `-deltaCents` and — only when the buffer fully absorbed it (`uncoveredCents === 0`) — that the other days stay the same; a partial cover says how much overflowed into the next days. The two numbers come **straight from `buildPiggyLedger`** (covered = `-deltaCents`, overflow = `uncoveredCents`); when the piggy covered nothing (`deltaCents >= 0`) **no** message shows (never a false claim). i18n `dashboard.piggy_covered_today` / `piggy_covered_partial` (pt/en/es). The covered/overflow contract is locked by the existing `piggy-ledger.test.ts` withdrawal/uncovered suite — **no math change (Â11)**.
- **E05 · DEC-325 · first-run "Descobrir" CTA.** Additive `discoverHintSeen?: boolean` (`domain/types/app-settings.ts`); seeded **false** for new installs (`createDefaultAppSettings`) and backfilled **true** for existing ones (`appSettingsRepository.get()` → `?? true`, so only a genuinely new install ever sees it). `DashboardPage.tsx`: while `discoverHintSeen === false`, the header shows an **expanded labelled "Descobrir o app"** CTA and **hides the empty bell**; `handleOpenDiscover` persists `discoverHintSeen: true` **and `reload()`s the shared app-data** (the context caches settings — an update alone is silent) so on return the header reverts to the compact compass icon + bell. i18n `discover.first_run_cta` (pt/en/es).
- **Tests (+ extended):** `dashboard-cards.test.ts` updated for the retired `daily_checkin` (default movable order, the `moveDashboardCard` swap, and the pairable list no longer reference it). `app-settings-repository.test.ts` +3 — new install defaults `discoverHintSeen=false`, legacy install reads back `true`, persisting `true` round-trips. ### G5 — Tools & capture: comparator in FAB (E07) · comparator photo fix (E10) · capture parity (E13) (done 2026-06-25)
- **E07 · DEC-327 · the cost-benefit comparator is a visible quick-action again.** `components/FAB.tsx`: moved the comparator from `group: 'other'` (the collapsed "Mais ações") to `group: 'plan'`, so it renders in the visible 2×2 smart-tools grid alongside Planejar gasto · Simular · Converter — filling the slot freed when the two split entries merged into one "Dividir" tile (DEC-311). Left it **non-`advanced`** (a shopping tool, like the converter) so it stays discoverable in simple mode too. Comment blocks updated; nothing deleted (Â9 — it only moved up the hierarchy).
- **E10 · DEC-330 · the comparator photo path no longer silently drops photos.** Root cause confirmed: `ComparatorPage.onPickedInput` read the **live** `event.target.files` and then set `event.target.value = ''` *before* `handlePickedFiles` ran — clearing the input empties its `FileList` in Chromium / Android WebView, so every picked photo vanished ("picker opens, then nothing"). **Fix:** snapshot to a `File[]` via `Array.from(event.target.files ?? [])` **before** the reset (the reliable pattern already used by `WiseImportPage` / the shared chooser); `handlePickedFiles` now takes `File[]`. Extracted the pure row mapping (`Row`, `MAX_ROWS`, `isFilledRow`, `outcomeToRow`) into `features/comparator/comparator-rows.ts` so the success→row / failure→review-row contract is unit-testable without React (no math touched — pure read, DEC-283).
- **E13 · DEC-333 · mic + camera are always offered, with one camera-or-gallery chooser.** **Assistant** (`features/assistant/AssistantSheet.tsx`): the camera button used to be hidden behind `photoEnabled` (cloud OCR opt-in) — it now renders **always**; tapping it while OCR is off opens the **same one-time consent** ReceiptScan uses (`receiptScan.consent_*`, reused — no new i18n), enables `cloudReceiptOcrEnabled`, `reload()`s the shared settings, then opens the shared `useImageSourceChooser` (camera vs gallery). Mic stays gated by a genuine capability check (`voiceAvailable`) and already asks RECORD_AUDIO permission on tap. **Group-split** (`features/group-split/GroupExpenseEditor.tsx`): the receipt prefill was a **camera-only** raw `<input capture="environment">` (no gallery) — replaced with the shared `useImageSourceChooser`, so it now offers the gallery too like every other capture surface (`useRef` import dropped, now unused). (The comparator's own multi-image picker already offers camera+gallery and can't use the single-file hook — noted as the one intentional exception.)
- **Tests (+ extended):** new `tests/unit/features/comparator/comparator-rows.test.ts` (6) — a confident read maps to a filled non-review row; a transport failure maps to an empty **review** row; a low-confidence/partial read still surfaces as a review row (no drop); a mixed batch preserves one row per photo; `isFilledRow` + `MAX_ROWS` guards. New `e2e/field-fixes-2-g5.spec.ts` (CI): the comparator shows in the FAB quick-actions and opens `/comparator` (E07); the assistant shows the camera with cloud OCR off and asks for consent on tap (E13). **Self-check:** cofrinho/insight/budget math untouched (Â11 — E07 is FAB ordering, E10 is a file-capture fix + pure-function extraction, E13 is capture UI + an opt-in toggle); the comparator stays a pure read that never creates an expense (DEC-283); expense logging never blocked; nothing deleted (Â9 — comparator moved up, group-split capture only *gained* the gallery option).
- **Gate close (G5):** full unit suite **2334 collected, 2332 pass** (only the 2 WebCrypto `split-live-loop` baseline; +6 vs G4) · `npx tsc --noEmit` clean · `npm run build` green (`index` 410.16 KB < 500 KB) · scrollbar lock intact (style-hygiene green). Bump 1.1.7-rc→**1.1.8-rc** (package.json + package-lock + app-version.ts + public/version.json `bundleUrl`→`1.1.8-rc.zip` + release note pt/en/es). Brain: DEC-327 + DEC-330 + DEC-333 → APPROVED. **Deploy:** commit + push `master` → Cloudflare Pages auto-build.

### G6 — Honesty & docs: reconcile pace × plan × piggy (E09) · backup texts/grouping/coach-marks (E12) (done 2026-06-25)
- **E09 · DEC-329 · "above pace" and a full cofrinho no longer read as a contradiction.** The field confusion: the copilot says "6 acima do ritmo / 12 acima do plano, fecha em 690" yet the cofrinho shows €32 saved — the user can't tell if the app is wrong. It isn't: the **piggy is the CUMULATIVE early savings** (what you banked on calm days), while **pace/plan are the FORWARD trend** of the recent days — independent lenses. **Fix is pure clarity, zero math (Â11):** in `CopilotPage.tsx`, the "where it's heading" block now renders **one extra line** only when the projection is *over* **and** `model.piggyBankCents > 0`, reading "mesmo assim, seu cofrinho guarda {amount}: é o que você poupou nos dias mais calmos; estar acima do ritmo é sobre os últimos dias — uma coisa não anula a outra" (i18n `copilot.where_piggy_note`, pt/en/es). Nothing else changed; the projection/pace/piggy numbers are byte-identical to baseline.
- **E09 reconciliation test.** New `tests/unit/domain/budget/pace-plan-piggy-reconciliation.test.ts` (4) pins the field fixture (€10/day ideal, four €0 days bank €40, then an €18 above-pace day withdraws €8 → **€32 left**): the cofrinho keeps the early savings even after a recent above-pace day; the buffer is internally consistent (`deposited − withdrawn === balance`, no double-count: 4000 − 800 = 3200); a projection-/allocation-over with real spend OK is **not** a real problem (`classifyBudgetSignal` → `projection_over`/`allocation_over`, `isRealProblem=false`); only a true overspend is a red (`real_over`). Proves the three lenses are mutually consistent — the explanation copy is backed by the math, not papering over it.
- **E12 · DEC-332 · the backup screen is honest and its tutorial matches the buttons.** (1) **Downloads, not Documentos** — `saveFileToDevice` writes to the **public Downloads** folder first (Documents only as fallback), so `backup.save_device_desc`/`save_device_done` (+ the stale code comments) now say Downloads (pt "Downloads" · en "Downloads" · es "Descargas"). (2) **No "JSON" jargon** — `backup.import_json` "Importar backup JSON"→"Importar backup"; the dead `export_json` key cleaned too. (3) **Grouped** — `BackupPage.tsx` now splits the actions under two headers: **"Enviar e guardar backup"** (send share · save to device · HTML summary · CSV · send-to-device P2P) and **"Importar e receber backup"** (import file · receive-from-device), so a button's direction is never ambiguous (i18n `backup.group_out`/`group_in`, pt/en/es). (4) **Fresh coach-marks** — `help-content.ts` backup topics now mirror the real buttons: `export`→"Enviar backup" (anchor `backup-export`), **new** `save`→"Salvar no aparelho" / Downloads (anchor `backup-save`), `csv`→"Exportar planilha (CSV)" (anchor `backup-csv`, was wrongly pointing at the send button), `import`→"Importar backup" (anchor `backup-import`); copy in pt/en/es drops every "JSON" mention.
- **E12 coach-mark audit (all tutorialed screens).** Cross-checked every `HELP_CONTENT` anchor against the live `data-help-anchor`s: funds (`funds-pool-list`, `funds-add`), planner (`planner-free-margin`, `planner-categories`), planned (`planned-list`/`-add`/`-buy`), wallets (`wallets-list`), phase_edit (`phase-edit-list`), outing (`outing-gauge`/`-quick-add`/`-items`/`-end`) — **all resolve**; their copy describes still-current concepts. **Only the backup screen was stale** (renamed buttons + a missing action), now fixed. Recorded in DEC-332.
- **Tests (+ extended):** `help-content.test.ts` (7) now also validates the new `save` topic resolves to title+body in all 3 languages (the registry contract); the new reconciliation test (4). `tsc` fix: the reconciliation test used `entries.at(-1)` (not in the project `lib` typings) → switched to index access. **Self-check:** cofrinho/insight/budget math untouched (Â11 — E09 is a pure read-only note + a consistency test, E12 is copy/grouping/coach-marks only); expense logging never blocked; nothing deleted (Â9 — backup actions only regrouped + relabelled, the tutorial gained a topic).
- **Gate close (G6):** full unit suite **2338 collected, 2336 pass** (only the 2 WebCrypto `split-live-loop` baseline; +4 vs G5) · `npx tsc --noEmit` clean · `npm run build` green (`index` 410.16 KB < 500 KB) · scrollbar lock intact (style-hygiene green). Bump 1.1.8-rc→**1.1.9-rc** (package.json + package-lock + app-version.ts + public/version.json `bundleUrl`→`1.1.9-rc.zip` + release note pt/en/es). ### G1 — D09 FAB closes on context switch + D06 Amigo voice-only (done 2026-06-25)
- **D09 · DEC-318 · the FAB never survives a context switch.** `BottomNav.tsx`: a `useEffect` on `location.pathname` sets `setIsFabOpen(false)` so any real navigation (tab tap, a card that routes, programmatic nav) closes the menu; the tab `onClick` ALSO closes it explicitly so tapping the tab you are **already on** (no pathname change) still closes — no exception. The scrim's own `onClick` keeps handling an outside tap. **AC:** an open FAB closes on tab tap / card nav / outside tap; never renders over the next screen.
- **D06 · DEC-317 · Amigo Sincero = only voice; factual extras become insights (no duplicate, math unchanged · Â11).** Pure adapter **`extraToInsight(extra)`** added to `domain/insights/insights.ts` (exported via `domain/insights/index.ts`) — maps a `HonestFriendExtra` (`piggy_movement|phase_progress|daily_left|top_category|receivable`) to a `DashboardInsight` with its own neutral kind/priority. `DashboardInsightKind` + `INSIGHT_PRIORITY` extended with the 5 factual kinds. `useDashboardModel.ts` now computes `insightsWithFactual` = existing insights ++ `filterHomeAmigoExtras(amigoExtras, visibleInsightKinds)` mapped through `extraToInsight` (reusing the existing dedup fn so a factual line never duplicates an analytical insight), and returns that as `insights`. The Amigo card is fed `extras={[]}` on both surfaces (`AmigoSinceroCard.tsx` default + `CopilotPage.tsx`), so its slide carousel only ever shows the opinion/verdict. `dashboard-format.ts` gained `INSIGHT_ICONS` + `formatInsightText` cases for the 5 kinds (neutral i18n keys `insight_piggy_in/out`, `insight_phase_progress`, `insight_daily_left`, `insight_top_category`, `insight_receivable` — pt-BR/en/es). `DashboardPage.handleInsightTap` routes `top_category`→filtered expenses, `receivable`→shared expenses.
- **Tests (+ extended):** new `domain/insights/extra-insights.test.ts` (extraToInsight maps each kind to the right neutral kind/icon/priority + tap target; dedup against visible insights via `filterHomeAmigoExtras` → no duplicate). Existing `honest-friend-extras` / insight builder tests still green (factual numbers identical to baseline — Â11). **Self-check:** cofrinho/insights math unchanged (only relocation of where a factual line is rendered); expense logging never blocked; nothing deleted (Â9 — Amigo keeps its voice, the data just moves to the carousel it belonged in).
- **E2E (CI):** `e2e/discovery-clarity-g1.spec.ts` — FAB closes on tab tap; FAB closes on scrim tap (stays on route); Amigo never renders a factual-extra slide tablist (home + copiloto). Playwright is CI-verified (Node 18.17.0 sandbox can't load its ESM config).

- **D11/D14 · DEC-313/314 · the day's saving shows ONE destination, never two.** Root cause: the check-in result (`DashboardCards.tsx` `daily_checkin`) rendered `checkin_redistribute` ("+X/day on the next days") **and** the `checkin_lens_calm` cofrinho caption at the same time — the double-count. New **pure** selector `domain/budget/saving-destination.ts` → `resolveSavingDestination({savedCents, piggyActive})` → `'piggy' | 'next_days' | 'none'` (zero React; math untouched, Â11). The check-in now renders a single labelled **"Destino da economia de hoje"** block (`[data-saving-destination]`): cofrinho active (`model.piggyLedger !== null`) → "você guarda X no cofrinho — ele segura os próximos dias"; no cofrinho → "diluído nos próximos dias: +Y/dia". The duplicate `piggy_bank` lens caption was removed; the cofrinho is never highlighted when the money didn't go to it. i18n `checkin_dest_title`/`checkin_dest_piggy` + reworded `checkin_redistribute` (pt-BR/en/es).
- **D12 · DEC-312 · official cofrinho rule documented + explained.** The rule is the implemented Model B (`buildPiggyLedger`, DEC-279/261) — **no math change** (Â11). Documented as `product-spec.md` §30 (deposit when under the day, cover-first when over, only `uncovered` cuts the next days; one destination). The plain-language copy already existed (`piggy_voice`) and is now reused in the hero sheet (see D13) and named in the check-in destination.
- **D13 · DEC-312 · "De onde vem?" covers the day's money.** The hero breakdown sheet (`DashboardSheets.tsx`) now appends a **"Como funciona o dinheiro do dia"** block after the FTS rows + phase map: cofrinho active → reuse `piggy_voice` (the rule) + `piggy_states_hint` (guardado/será guardado/simulado); no cofrinho → new `hero_day_nextdays` (the day's slice dilutes into the next days). Covers livre × valor do dia × gastou menos/mais × cofrinho × próximos dias × guardado/simulado.
- **D10 · DEC-316 · compact "posso gastar".** `AskToSpendShortcut.tsx` went from a full-width `w-full px-4 py-3` card to a discreet inline pill (`inline-flex px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs`), still right under "free today" and still priming the simulator. Nothing removed (Â9).
- **Tests (+ extended):** new `domain/budget/saving-destination.test.ts` (4: piggy when active+saved, next_days when no cofrinho, none when saved≤0, only-ever-one-of-three). `piggy-ledger.test.ts` (19) re-run green → cofrinho math identical to baseline (Â11). New `e2e/discovery-clarity-g4.spec.ts`: "Sem gastos" check-in → exactly one `[data-saving-destination]`, piggy XOR next-days never both; hero "De onde vem?" → day-money explainer visible; compact "posso gastar" chip → `/simulator`. **Self-check:** no total/balance changed (only relocation/labelling of where the destination is shown); expense logging untouched; nothing deleted (Â9 — the lens spotlight stays for outing/night; only the redundant piggy caption went).
- **Gate close (G4):** full unit suite **2293 collected, 2291 pass** (only the 2 WebCrypto `split-live-loop` baseline; +4 saving-destination) · `npx tsc --noEmit` clean · `npm run build` green (`index` 409.28 KB < 500 KB). Bump 1.1.0-rc→**1.1.1-rc** (package.json + app-version.ts + public/version.json `bundleUrl`→`1.1.1-rc.zip` + release note pt/en/es). ### G5 — Phase-scoped pots: off-phase pots out of focus (still selectable) · create flow separates Event × Pote/Fundo (done 2026-06-25)
- **Root-cause confirmed (re-read of §6 D15 before editing).** A "Pote" is a `global` pool with optional `dateStart/dateEnd/goalCents` (`types/budget-pool.ts`); `linked_phases` pools are the trecho operational budgets. The Home `funds_summary` (`DashboardCards.tsx`) already showed only `selectVisiblePots` (GATE-3 D8: dated pots hidden until their owner trecho is active or the D-7 window opens) — but those off-phase pots simply **vanished** from the Home (the comment said "the full list lives on /viagem"), so they weren't discoverable. The expense fund picker (`getAvailablePoolsForPhase`) already lists **every** global pot, and `createPlannedExpense` already gives a standalone Pote `dateStart = null` (no countdown). So D15 = surface the off-phase pots in a collapsed area + lock the selectable invariant + sharpen the create-flow copy. **No math change (Â11).**
- **D15a · DEC-314 · "Potes de outras fases" (collapsed), off-phase out of focus.** New **pure** `selectOtherPhasePots(pots, activePhase, today)` in `domain/budget/pots.ts` (exported via `domain/budget/index.ts`) — the exact complement of `selectVisiblePots` among **dated** global pots (a dateless/ambient pot is never "other phase"). `useDashboardModel.ts` exposes `otherPhasePotSummaries`; `DashboardCards.tsx` renders a collapsed **`OtherPhasePotsSection`** (`[data-other-phase-pots]`) under the focus pots — a toggle + a one-line hint when collapsed; expanding lists each off-phase pot (name · date range · remaining) → tap opens `/funds`. Nothing deleted (Â9 — the pot just moves down the hierarchy). i18n `dashboard.pots_other_phases`/`_hint` (pt-BR/en/es).
- **D15b · DEC-314 · off-phase pot stays selectable.** No code change needed — `getAvailablePoolsForPhase` returns all `scope === 'global'` pots regardless of date/phase. Locked by a new regression test (the same pot that is hidden from the Burgos Home is present in the expense fund list).
- **D15c · DEC-315 · creation separates Event × Pote/Fundo (no countdown on a pote).** The single `PlanExpenseSheet` "Planejar um gasto" door already forks on Q1 (has a date → Event; no date → Pote/Fundo) and routes via `createPlannedExpense` (a standalone pote carries no date → no countdown is ever possible). Sharpened the Q1 copy (`q1_yes_desc` now "evento com contagem regressiva", `q1_no`/`q1_no_desc` now "Não tem data" / "uma compra ou um pote/fundo — sem contagem") and added a `[data-plan-kind-note]` line stating the rule (event = "faltam X dias"; pote = sem contagem, recebe gastos a qualquer momento). i18n `plan.q1_note_event`/`plan.q1_note_pote` (pt-BR/en/es).
- **Tests (+ extended):** `domain/budget/visible-pots.test.ts` → **+7** (selectOtherPhasePots: lists Tomorrowland under Burgos; drops it once Eurotrip active / inside the D-7 window; never an ambient pot; excludes soft-deleted & non-global; exact complement of selectVisiblePots; **D15b** hidden-but-selectable). `domain/orchestrators/plan-orchestrators.test.ts` → **+2** (a standalone Pote carries no date and creates no occurrence — never a countdown; an Event always carries a dated occurrence). **Self-check:** cofrinho/insights/totals untouched (Â11 — only which pots are shown where); expense logging never blocked (the fund list is unchanged, only the Home focus filters); nothing deleted (Â9). E2E: new `e2e/discovery-clarity-g5.spec.ts` (create-door Event×Pote toggle/no-countdown; create a +30-day pot → absent from Home focus, in the collapsed area, still in the fund selector) — CI-verified.
- **Gate close (G5):** full unit suite **2302 collected, 2300 pass** (only the 2 WebCrypto `split-live-loop` baseline; +9 vs G4) · `npx tsc --noEmit` clean · `npm run build` green (`index` 409.28 KB < 500 KB). Bump 1.1.1-rc→**1.1.2-rc** (package.json + app-version.ts + public/version.json `bundleUrl`→`1.1.2-rc.zip` + release note pt/en/es). Brain: DEC-314 + DEC-315 → APPROVED. **Deploy:** commit + push `master` → Cloudflare Pages auto-build.

### G6 *(optional, taken on)* — D07 per-item price history (done 2026-06-25)
- **Scope decision.** Of the two G6 items: **D05** (single groups screen) was already delivered in G3 (the duplicate generic "Group split" door is gated by `hasGroupSettle`, with a "Ver todas" link to `/groups`) — re-read confirms no further low-risk finishing is warranted, so G6 = **D07 only**. New **DEC-320** records D07's shape + why it is exempt from the DEC-319 guide/help coverage contract (it is a contextual insight with no standalone entry point, like the anchor hint DEC-128).
- **D07 · DEC-320 · pure price history.** New **pure, zero-token, offline** `domain/shopping/price-history.ts` → `buildPriceHistory({ current, transactions })` (the twin of the unit-price comparator DEC-283/284). It matches the same item by **`normalizeItemName`** (NFD accent-strip + lowercase + whitespace-collapse) and compares in the trip's **base currency** (`baseCurrencyAmountCents`, so a buy abroad lines up with one at home). Only real, non-deleted expenses with a positive base amount count; **<2 matches → null** (no history). Returns `count / minCents / maxCents / avgCents (rounded) / currentCents / deltaFromAvgCents (signed) / isCheapest / isPriciest / points[]` (newest-first, the current buy flagged). Exported via `domain/shopping/index.ts`. **No schema, no migration, no math touched (Â11).**
- **D07 UI.** `features/expenses/ExpenseDetailPage.tsx` computes `buildPriceHistory({ current: tx, transactions })` and, when non-null, renders a `PriceHistoryCard` (`[data-price-history]`) between the details card and the shared block: a min/avg/max stat row + a one-line plain verdict (cheapest / priciest / "{delta} above|below your average" / "same price as always") + a tap-to-expand list of every past purchase (date + price, the current one highlighted). All copy via `t()` — new `expenses.price_history_*` keys (title/count/min/avg/max/cheapest/priciest/above_avg/below_avg/same/see_all/hide/this_one) in pt-BR/en/es. A `data-quickadd-description` hook was added to the Quick Add description input (E2E targeting; mirrors the `[data-plan-kind-note]`/`[data-other-phase-pots]` precedent).
- **Tests (+ new):** new `domain/shopping/price-history.test.ts` — **12** (normalizeItemName accent/case/space equivalence; guards: <2 matches → null, non-expense → null, empty name → null; math with verified cents [300,500,400]→min300/avg400/max500, signed delta, cheapest/priciest flags, avg rounding; matching set excludes other items + soft-deleted + non-expense + zero-base; cross-currency via base amount; points newest-first with the current flagged). **Self-check:** cofrinho/insights/totals untouched (Â11 — D07 is a read-only derivation over existing transactions); expense logging never blocked; nothing deleted (Â9 — the card only appears when there's history). E2E: new `e2e/discovery-clarity-g6.spec.ts` (an item bought twice → price-history card with count 2, min/avg/max labels, expand → "this purchase" flagged; a unique demo item → no card) — CI-verified (Playwright can't load under Node 18.17.0).
- **Gate close (G6):** full unit suite **2314 collected, 2312 pass** (only the 2 WebCrypto `split-live-loop` baseline; +12 price-history) · `npx tsc --noEmit` clean · `npm run build` green (`index` 409.28 KB < 500 KB). Bump 1.1.2-rc→**1.1.3-rc** (package.json + app-version.ts + public/version.json `bundleUrl`→`1.1.3-rc.zip` + release note pt/en/es). Brain: DEC-320 → APPROVED. **Deploy:** commit + push `master` → Cloudflare Pages auto-build.

## Field-feedback fixes wave (2026-06-25) — 1.0.1-rc — ✅ SHIPPED (web/OTA)

> Source: Julio field feedback after the 1.0.0-rc Coherence & Tricount wave. Six items, all root-caused in the code (no report existed in `docs/reports/INDEX.md`). One continuous session, single deploy. Most recent first; the Coherence & Tricount state is preserved below.

- **Phase/fund on import was wrong (receipt AI/photo + Wise CSV)** — root cause: `ReceiptScanPage`, `WiseImportPage`, `SplitPage` and the simulator all defaulted to the FIRST `linked_phases` pool (and "today" for the phase) instead of the phase that owns the expense's date. Fix: derive the phase from the expense date (`resolveActivePhase` / per-row `classifyWiseRows`) and resolve its operational pool with `selectActivePhasePool` (the same fn the dashboard uses). Added a reusable **`PhaseChargePicker`** (auto-by-date default + manual override) to the receipt, split and Wise review screens. Wise orchestrators (`commitWiseImport`/`commitWiseTransfers`) gained `poolByPhaseId` (per-row pool) + `forcePhaseId` (import-wide override). +4 unit tests (wise routing + force override).
- **No batch edit of phase/fund** — root cause: a receipt becomes a Session holding N transactions; the list only let you edit item by item. Fix: new pure orchestrators `moveTransactionsToPhaseBatch` (standalone, e.g. Wise) and `moveOutingSessionsToPhaseBatch` (a whole note/outing → session + every item), wired into the `ExpenseListPage` selection mode on BOTH tabs (Gastos = standalone expenses, Saídas = sessions). Phase + its operational pool move together. +2 unit tests; i18n pt/en/es.
- **Multi-day event reserve dumped on the first day** — `indexPlanByDay` (`allowance-map.ts`) now spreads an occurrence's reserve evenly across its days (integer-cents, remainder on the last day), mirroring `burndown.ts`. So "available per day" shows the daily average. +3 unit tests.
- **Simulator showed another phase's balance** — `SimulatorPage` now picks `selectActivePhasePool(pools, links, activePhase.id)` instead of `linkedPools[0]`.
- **Input focus border disliked** — `globals.css`: inputs/textarea/select/contenteditable now `outline:none; box-shadow:none` on focus (supersedes DEC-305's neutral ring; buttons/links/cards keep the keyboard `:focus-visible` ring). Updated the C25 style-hygiene test to assert the removal (acknowledged WCAG 2.4.7 trade-off; mobile-first touch app).
- **Home insights cap 4 → 6** — `HOME_INSIGHTS_CAP` in `home-insights.ts`.
- **Quality**: `tsc --noEmit` clean · `npm run build` green (`index` 406 KB < 500 KB) · **unit 2271/2273** (the 2 failures are the documented `split-live-loop` WebCrypto baseline — `crypto.subtle` needs Node 22, unavailable in this Node 18.17.0 sandbox; pass in CI). Bumped `package.json` + `app-version.ts` + `public/version.json` (`bundleUrl`→`1.0.1-rc.zip`) + release notes pt/en/es. Deploy: commit + push `master` → Cloudflare Pages auto-build (`build:pages`).

### G2 — Outing×Item grouped reading (done 2026-06-24)
- **C01 · DEC-296 · an outing is ONE entity in every summary; only text search itemises.** The bug: clicking a category (or a dashboard drill → `/expenses?category=…`) set the old `isBrowsing` flag false under **any** filter, so the rollup turned off and a single receipt exploded into loose item rows — the exact "Mercado virou 6 linhas" scare. The domain (`expense-feed.ts` `buildSessionFeed(txs, sessionById, collapse)`) was already correct (Â11 — pure, collapses a session's txs into one entry with `totalCents = Σ matching txs`); **only the caller computed the flag wrong.**
  - **Fix (`ExpenseListPage.tsx`):** `collapseSessions = !searchQuery` — collapse a session into one feed row whenever the user is **not** doing a free-text search, even with a category/profile/place/wallet filter active. Only a **text search** itemises (then the user is hunting a specific line).
  - **Honest-to-the-filter copy (DEC-296):** under a category filter the matched txs are pre-filtered upstream, so the collapsed row's `totalCents` is already the **filtered subtotal** and `txs.length` the **filtered count**. `SessionRollupRow` now takes `filterCategory` and reads `"N itens nesta categoria"` (new i18n `expenses.outing_items_in_category`, pt/en/es) instead of plain `"N itens"`; a tap still routes to `/outings/<id>/review` = the **full** outing (full total + every item). Nothing lost.

### G6 — Settlement lifecycle + universal share feedback (done 2026-06-24)
- **C05 · DEC-303 · every share action gives visible feedback.** `TripWrappedSheet.handleShare` only toasted on `'failed'`, so on desktop the silent download read as "nothing happens". New **pure** `shareOutcomeToast(outcome)` (in `utils/share-card.ts`) maps a `ShareCardOutcome` → `{messageKey, tone}`: `shared`→success, `downloaded`→success (the desktop notice), `failed`→danger, `aborted`→**null** (user cancelled, no toast). Both `TripWrappedSheet` and `TripHubPage` now route through it (one language across every share surface). New i18n `share.shared_toast` (×3). Canvas taint: `renderShareCard` draws only text/shapes (no cross-origin images) and `canvas.toBlob` resolves null on failure → the `!blob` path already fires the failed toast, so a tainted/empty canvas can never read as success.

New **pure** `resolveShareStage({confirmationStatus,isPaid})` → `pending | confirmed | paid | rejected`: rejected wins; pending = awaiting accept; confirmed = accepted but not paid; **paid** = accepted AND marked paid (the only "done" stage). `SharedExpenseRow` (owner's `ParticipantShare`, has `isPaid`) and `StatementGroupRow` now render the stage; `StatementLine` gained an additive `isPaid` (set by `buildParticipantStatement` from the share; internal/UI only — the mirror payload has its own schema, untouched). New pill style `paid` (solid `bg-success text-white`) + i18n `shared.status_paid` (×3) + the `status_hint` now spells the full cycle (pending → confirmed → paid). Connection states (connected/waiting/offline) + connected-pending group + settlement standing were already present (preserved).
- **Test (+8):** `share-outcome-toast` unit (4: shared/downloaded success, failed danger, aborted null) + `share-stage` unit (4: rejected wins, pending even if flagged paid, confirmed unpaid, paid). Existing splitting+sync suites green after the additive `StatementLine.isPaid` (4 mirror-cycle fixtures + the grouping `mkLine` factory updated to `isPaid:false`). **AC:** every share action runs a visible result; the settle screen makes each person's accept→pay cycle explicit; red only on a real problem (rejected), green solid only when actually paid.

### G8 — Tricount group-split (C23, DEC-297) — IN PROGRESS (2026-06-24)
- **m1 · aggregate + event CRUD + participants + manual expenses (equal & custom) + multi-payer + balances/transfers.** New **pure** `domain/group-split/*` — a brand-new aggregate (Council C-C/§16 Q4), deliberately separate from the single-bill `SplitSession`: `GroupSplitEvent` (name, currency, optional `tripId`, owner, participants, expenses, status) with money as integer cents and all math pure. Functions: factories (`createGroupSplitEvent` seeds the owner, `createGroupParticipant`, `buildGroupExpense`), immutable mutations (`addParticipant`/`removeParticipant`+`canRemoveParticipant` guard [never owner, never someone inside an expense], `addExpense`/`updateExpense`/`removeExpense`, `setParticipantPayment`, `claimParticipant`, `setGroupStatus`), and the math (Â11): `expenseShares` (equal=`splitEqually` deterministic remainder; custom=given amounts, short remainder absorbed by the payer so Σ shares==amount), `groupTotalCents`, `computeGroupBalances` (paid/share/net per person, Σ net==0), `computeGroupTransfers` (greedy min-transfer settle, mirrors trip settle-up), `isGroupSettled`/`everyDebtorConfirmed`, `validateGroupExpense`.
- **Data layer (Â11 — justified migration).** New `GroupSplitRecord` (wraps the event + sync metadata; `id`===`event.id`; indexed `tripId`/`status` projections). **Dexie V12** adds the `groupSplitEvents` table — a *brand-new table* (no `upgrade()`, every existing row untouched; `migration-v12.test.ts` proves it + round-trips a record). `GroupSplitRepository` (extends `BaseRepository`): `createFromEvent`/`saveEvent` (refresh projections)/`listEvents`(trip-scoped, newest first)/`getEvent`. **Backup v7→v8**: `groupSplitEvents` joins `BackupData`+`BACKUP_TABLE_KEYS`+the Zod schema+`normalizeBackupToV8` (older files default to `[]`), so a device restore keeps group history (same retention policy as `splitSessions`). The export/import orchestrator is data-driven from `BACKUP_TABLE_KEYS` → no change needed there.
- **Orchestrator** `domain/orchestrators/group-split-orchestrators.ts`: `createGroupSplit` (seeds owner from the trip owner + persists), `persistGroupSplit` (the single write seam pages call after a pure mutation), `deleteGroupSplit` (soft-delete, Â9).
- **UI** (`features/group-split/`): `GroupSplitListPage` (`/groups`) — every group newest-first (owner-net framed: "você recebe/deve"), inline create (name + currency, owner reused from trip). `GroupSplitDetailPage` (`/groups/:id`) — live total, balances (gets/owes/even), **who-pays-whom** transfers, expenses list, people add/remove, mark-settled/reopen, delete. `GroupExpenseEditor` (reuses `BottomSheet`) — description/amount/payer chips/who-shares checkboxes + **equal⇄custom** toggle with a live per-person preview (folds m2 *and* m4 into one form; the domain already supports both). Entry point: a card on `SharedExpensesPage` (always visible → discoverable). Routes registered in `router.tsx` (`/groups`, `/groups/:id` inside AppShell).
- **i18n:** new `group_split.*` namespace ×3 langs (pt-BR/en/es), zero hardcoded UI text; reuses `common.*`/`onboarding.currency`.
- **Test (+12):** `group-split.test.ts` (10 — equal/custom remainder, multi-payer balances Σ net==0, min-transfer settle = 2 transfers to the creditor, settled state, remove-guards, validation matrix, payment lifecycle) + `migration-v12.test.ts` (2 — additive table + record round-trip). Backup tests updated to v8 (`backup`/`normalize-v5`/`migration-v4`). **AC covered:** create group, add people, add expenses (manual), divide (equal **and** custom), multi-payer, see who-pays-whom — all with the Σ invariant. Remaining m3 (AI/receipt inside the event), m5 (public `/g/` link + claim), m6 (settle-up sync + guest no-app + Tricount help) + **1.0.0-rc** ship at gate close.
- **Checkpoint (m1):** full unit suite **2243 collected, 2241 pass** (only the 2 documented WebCrypto `split-live-loop` baseline fails; +12 G8 tests) · `npx tsc --noEmit` clean · `npm run build` green (`index` 403.27 KB < 500 KB). No deploy yet (deploy is at gate close with the 1.0.0-rc bump).
- **m3 · AI + receipt reading inside the event.** A Tricount books **one expense per bill** (per-item detail is the single-bill SplitSession's job), so both AI paths return the same minimal prefill (`description` + `amountCents` + `category`) and the user still confirms payer + split. New feature boundary `features/group-split/group-ai.ts` reuses the existing cloud routes — **receipt**: `extractReceiptViaCloud` (`/ocr`) → `summarizeReceiptTotal` + `dominantReceiptCategory`; **text**: `requestAssistantIntents` (`/assistant`) with a minimal low-sensitivity pack (no people/wallets — the group resolves the payer by hand) → first money intent. Never throws; every failure folds to a typed error (`not_configured`/`rate_limited`/`offline`/`empty`/`failed`). The `GroupExpenseEditor` (new-expense only) gained two opt-in triggers — **"Escanear nota"** (camera/file → fill) and **"Pedir pra IA"** (inline phrase → fill) — gated by the same settings as the rest of the app (`cloudReceiptOcrEnabled`/`aiQuickEntryEnabled`); a filled expense carries `source: 'receipt'|'ai'`. New i18n `group_split.ai_*` (×3). **Test (+7):** `group-ai` (receipt summarize/rate-limit/empty; text amount-extraction/no-amount/empty-skips-cloud/offline) with the cloud + image boundaries mocked. **AC:** add a group expense by photo or phrase, then divide it like any other.
- **Checkpoint (m3):** `group-ai` **7/7** · `npx tsc --noEmit` clean · lints clean. Remaining: m5 (public `/g/` link + claim) + m6 (settle-up sync + guest no-app + Tricount help) → **1.0.0-rc** at gate close.
- **m5 · public `/g/` link + claim (pick name) + mark/confirm paid.** Reuses the EXACT same encrypted transport as the bill-split live table (DEC-207 capability model): worker stores opaque ciphertext, the AES key lives only in the link fragment. New **pure** domain: `group-split/share-payload.ts` (`buildGroupSharePayload`/`parseGroupSharePayload` — Zod-validates the whole `GroupSplitEvent`, corrupt blob → `null`) and `group-split/claim-response.ts` (`buildGroupClaimResponse`/`parseGroupClaimResponse` + **owner-authoritative** `reduceGroupClaims`). The guest claim is a *snapshot* — `{claimedParticipantId, markedPaid}` — and the reducer: binds one device→one slot (releases a stale slot on switch), **never** lets a guest claim the owner slot or set `confirmed`, and **never** downgrades a slot the owner already confirmed (only the owner moves off `confirmed`). New transport seam `features/group-split/group-link.ts` mirrors `live-link.ts` (`publishGroupSplit`/`republishGroupSplit`/`revokeGroupSplit`/`pullGroupClaims`/`fetchGroupResponses`/`fetchGroupSplit`/`postGroupClaim`/`buildGroupSplitLink`) over the same `crypto`+`share-client`; owner creds persist in a localStorage map keyed by event id (the owner runs many events), guest identity reuses `live-link`'s `getGuestActorId`/`getGuestName`. New **`/g/:id`** route (outside BootGate, beside `/t/`) → `GroupClaimPage`: pick your name, see your net (owe/get-back + the exact transfer), **mark as paid** → owner confirms; live via signal + 6 s poll floor. Owner side (`GroupSplitDetailPage`): a contextual Share card (publish → OS share/copy → re-share/stop), a claims poller that folds guest snapshots in (idempotent — only a real change re-publishes), a "joined via link" tag, and a per-debtor settle control (`Marcar recebido`/`Confirmar`/`Recebido✓` revertible — worded, amber/green, never red; DEC-299/Â9). New domain URL builder `buildGroupSplitUrl` + `GROUP_SPLIT_PATH_PREFIX='/g/'` (worker unchanged — reuses the `/share` routes per DEC-297). New i18n `group_split.*` (share/payment) + new `group_claim.*` namespace ×3 langs.
- **Test (+11):** `group-link.test.ts` — payload round-trip (build→JSON→parse equal; negative revision floored; corrupt→null) + claim build/parse (blank name→"Convidado"; malformed→null) + `reduceGroupClaims` matrix (bind+escalate to marked; owner slot un-claimable; confirmed never downgraded; un-mark while only marked; latest-per-device + stale-slot release on switch; idempotent re-fold). **AC covered:** share a group, a guest opens `/g/` and picks their name, sees what they owe + who to pay, marks paid; the owner sees the claim, confirms receipt; Σ/balances unchanged (Â11). Domain stays pure (zero React in the math/reducer); money in cents.
- **Checkpoint (m5):** group-split domain **21/21** (10 m1 + 11 m5) · `npx tsc --noEmit` clean · lints clean. Remaining: m6 (settle-up sync + saved history + guest no-app + Tricount help) → **1.0.0-rc** at gate close.
- **m6 · settle-up sync + history + guest-no-app + Tricount help.** **Settle-up sync (DEC-306, council-decided this session — Architect/Risk dominant: a *contained read-only bridge*, not a ledger merge).** New **pure** `domain/group-split/settle-bridge.ts` `groupSplitToDebts(event, tripBaseCurrency)` projects the group's minimum transfers onto **trip-participant space**, keeping only edges where BOTH sides carry a `linkedParticipantId` (an app person tied to a trip Participant) and the debtor isn't already `confirmed` in-group; a currency guard drops groups whose currency ≠ trip base (never sum unlike cents). `groupSplitsToTripDebts(events, tripId, base)` flattens many trip-linked events (standalone/foreign-currency contribute nothing). The trip money ledger is **never mutated** — the settle action stays in the group (mark→confirm, m5) as the single source of truth; the bridge output is consumed **read-only**, folded through the existing `summarizeOwnerDebts` for an additive "Em divisões de grupo" section on `SharedExpensesPage` (owner net + per-group deep links + a note that confirming in-group is what clears it). Affordance to make an event trip-linked: `GroupSplitDetailPage` now offers **quick-add chips of the trip's people** (creates a `connected` participant carrying `linkedParticipantId`) + a "pagamentos confirmados aqui entram no acerto da viagem" note. **Guest-no-app** is already satisfied by m5's `/g/` claim board (pick name → see balance → mark paid, zero install). **Saved history** is m1's `groupSplitEvents` table + backup v8 (events persist + restore). **Tricount help (C13 deferred item):** new `group_split` KB article (route `/groups`, section people, q/a/steps ×3) now that the route is live — the catalog coverage test (every route registered + copy in 3 langs) stays green.
- **Test (+5):** `settle-bridge.test.ts` — bridges only edges where both sides are trip-linked (unlinked Carla dropped; Bruno→Ana in trip space); drops the edge once the debtor is `confirmed`; keeps it while only `marked`; bridges nothing on currency mismatch; `groupSplitsToTripDebts` flattens only trip-scoped same-currency events (standalone excluded). **AC covered:** a trip-linked group's confirmed payments surface in the trip settle-up read-only, without touching the trip ledger; unlinked/foreign/unconfirmed correctly excluded.

### G5 — Voice & moments (done 2026-06-24)
- **M16b · DEC-291 · honest-friend voices that actually differ.** The four Amigo Sincero voices (padrão/zen/durão/econômico) drew from a phrase bank that was too samey, so switching tone barely changed the lead line (§16-Q6). Rewrote the **whole `amigo_voice` bank** across pt-BR/en/es so each voice is unmistakable: **zen** genuinely gentle ("respira e curte o momento"), **durão** genuinely blunt ("estourou. Sem desculpa: corta o excesso agora"), **padrão** warm-and-straight, **econômico** framed around the reserve. The verdict logic is untouched — the voice only flavors the short lead line (M16b is **voice, not density**; M16-density stays DEFERRED). **AC:** for the same state the tones read clearly different; the blunt one is bluntest. **Tests:** `honest-friend-voice` — distinctness across **all 3 locales** (same band+index → 4 distinct lines; no line repeats within a voice) + coverage extended pt-BR→all locales. **Commit:** `c5e7632`.
- **M17-lite · DEC-292 · light closing recap reusing OutingReview.** No "the end that makes you proud" moment existed; ending an outing just fired a toast → dashboard (§16-Q5). Added a **warm peak-end recap sheet** on close (the full closing screen stays ⏳ post-validation, kit §15/D). **Domain (pure):** `domain/outing/outing-recap.ts` `buildOutingRecap({transactions,startedAt,endedAt,targetCents,now?})` → `{totalCents,itemCount,durationMin,targetCents,vsTargetCents,outcome}` with `outcome: under|on|over|no_target` (a vs-target read needs a real target AND real spend, per DEC-173), plus `recapHeadlineKey` + `formatRecapDuration`. This is the **single source** for the recap figures — `SessionReview`'s existing chips now read from it too, and the duplicated local `formatDurationShort` is removed (one site repointed). **UI:** `ClosingRecapSheet.tsx` — a `BottomSheet` with an outcome-tuned headline, the final total, the same duration/rounds/vs-target chips, a primary "Concluir"→dashboard and a secondary "Ver resumo completo"→`/outings/:id/review` (reuses the read-only `OutingReviewPage` — **no new heavy screen**). Wired in `OutingPage.handleConfirmEnd`: the recap state is set before clearing the session and rendered as an early-return over a dimmed background; dismiss navigates on. **AC:** closing shows a light recap with real data; nothing heavy added; expense recording never blocked (Â9). **Tests:** `outing-recap` unit (11: totals/itemCount/duration, under/on/over/no-target classification, deleted+non-expense ignored, clock fallback, never-negative duration, headline-key mapping, duration formatting). +6 `outing.recap_*` i18n keys ×3 langs. **Commit:** `a740043`.
- **Regression watch:** verdict logic and money/expense paths untouched; the recap refactor reuses the exact prior figures (`buildOutingRecap` mirrors the old inline calc, proven by the unchanged `SessionReview` chips + green outing suite); no schema/worker change; nothing removed (Â9 — the discard flow and its toast are intact, ending still reaches the dashboard one tap away).

Added a **pure** `resolveSettlementStanding(debts, sharedExpenseCount, settlementCount)` in `domain/splitting` → `{hasActivity, outstandingCents, allSettled}`; `allSettled` is true ONLY when real splitting happened (a shared expense or a recorded settlement exists) AND `outstandingCents === 0` (sums positive debts only, so a settled/negative pair never blocks it). The hero's even-branch now renders a proud "Tudo acertado ✓" seal (`verified` icon, success tint, `data-all-settled-seal`) when `allSettled`, else the neutral "Tudo em dia". **AC:** the seal appears exactly when the group balance zeros after activity; never a false positive on a fresh trip; the recebe/deve read is always derivable. **Tests:** `settlement-standing` unit (6: seal only with activity+zero, not while outstanding, no false positive on a new trip, settlement-only activity, positive-debts-only sum, multi-debt outstanding total). +2 i18n keys (`shared.all_settled_title/_hint`) ×3 langs.

  - **(FB-03 · DEC-265) Registro de local ligado por padrão (emenda à ÂNCORA 8).** `createDefaultAppSettings` (seed) passou a nascer com `locationCaptureEnabled: true` + `locationDefaultNoticeAcknowledged: false` → **só instalação NOVA** começa com o tagging ligado e vê **uma vez** o aviso transparente. **Privacidade preservada:** o GPS continua só sendo lido com permissão do SO, e é um toque pra desligar nas Configurações. **Retrocompat (AC4):** o backfill de leitura do `appSettingsRepository.get()` mantém `locationCaptureEnabled ?? false` (um restore/registro antigo NUNCA vira ON na leitura — a escolha prévia do usuário vence) e `locationDefaultNoticeAcknowledged ?? true` (instalações existentes leem como já-cientes → nunca veem aviso de mudança que não houve). Campo novo opcional, não-indexado → sem migração. UI: `LocationDefaultNoticeCard` no topo da Home (após o banner de demo), com "Entendi" (`handleAckLocationNotice`) e "Configurações" (`handleOpenLocationSettings`); pulado no demo e em todo install existente. i18n `dashboard.location_notice_*` pt/en/es.
  - **(FB-25 · DEC-274) Planejador: abrir não grava + guarda do Dia a dia.** `PlannerPage` ganhou `userEditedRef` (default `false`, **resetado a cada (re)hidratação**): o efeito de persistência debounced agora exige `hydratedRef.current && userEditedRef.current` — abrir a tela (hidratar → `setStates` semeando dos defaults dos perfis) **nunca** cria `ScenarioPlan`/allocations. Só uma **edição real** marca `userEditedRef = true` (os 6 caminhos: `updateCount`, `toggleLock`, `applyPreset`, `applyRecommendation`, `handleSetPriority`, `handleAddCategory`). Guarda de capacidade: viagem `ongoing` (Dia a dia) cai num **early-return** com `EmptyState` ("planejador é para viagens com datas" → CTA voltar pra Home) antes de qualquer hidratação/escrita — Dia a dia não tem fases/datas pra montar cenários. i18n `planner.ongoing_title/body/cta` pt/en/es.
  - **(FB-27 · DEC-277) Registrar reembolso recebido (método estruturado).** Reusa o `Settlement` existente (debtor→creditor) — **nenhum tipo novo**. Tipo `SettlementMethod = PaymentMethodKind | 'cash'` + `SETTLEMENT_METHOD_KINDS`/`SETTLEMENT_METHOD_ICONS` em `payment-methods.ts` (o `cash` é **só do acerto** — `PAYMENT_METHOD_KINDS`/o editor "como te pagar" da DEC-244 ficam intocados → zero regressão). `Settlement.method?: SettlementMethod | null` (opcional, não-indexado, sobrevive ao backup pelo `syncedRecordSchema.passthrough()` — mesmo padrão do `externalRef`). `createSettlement(...)` ganhou 6º parâmetro opcional `method = null` (call-sites e o import Wise/DEC-200 byte-idênticos). UI no `SharedExpensesPage`: a sheet de acerto ganhou um seletor de chips opcional (Pix/Wise/conta/dinheiro/outro, toque de novo p/ limpar) passado ao `createSettlement`; o histórico de acertos mostra o método (ícone + rótulo) quando registrado. A redução do saldo devido (AC2/AC4) já era do `calculateDebts` — sem mudança de matemática. i18n `shared.settle_method_label` + `payment.kind_cash` pt/en/es.

- **🟢 0.99.46 (web/OTA + WORKER) — Field Feedback · GATE 7 — Resiliência da IA (degradação graciosa) + Admin v3 (FB-26/FB-19/FB-17/FB-21/FB-18).** Source: master plan §4 Gate 7 + councils C7/C8/C10 + **DEC-272/273/274/276**; protocolo C15/DEC-280 (sessão única).
  - **(FB-26 · DEC-276, centro) Degradação graciosa quando o free tier do Groq estoura.** **Worker:** nos 3 handlers de IA (`/assistant`·`/ocr`·`/transcribe`) o 429 do Groq agora responde corpo estruturado `{ error, aiUnavailable:true, retryAfterSec, scope:'minute'|'day' }` derivado dos headers reais (`parseGroqResetSeconds` lê `retry-after`/`x-ratelimit-reset-*` em forma numérica OU duração "1m30s"/"880ms"; prefere `retry-after`, senão o maior dos resets; `scope='day'` quando >300s). **Caminho feliz 100% intocado.** **Domínio PURO `domain/assistant/ai-cooldown.ts`** (12 testes): `cooldownFromBody` (coage entrada não-confiável; default honesto 30s; cap 1 dia; arredonda; `day` contraditório com nº pequeno → ≥60s), `defaultCooldown`, `cooldownRemainingSec`, `isCoolingDown`. **Transports** (`ai-assistant`/`ai-ocr`/`ai-transcribe`) leem o corpo do 429 via `readCooldown` e anexam `cooldown` ao outcome `rate_limited`. **`useAssistant`**: estado de cooldown (ref + tick 1/s que auto-limpa no fim), `enterCooldown`/`blockedByCooldown` **travam os 3 gatilhos** (texto/voz/foto) enquanto cooling; expõe `aiCooldownSec`. **`AssistantSheet`**: `ErrorArea` mostra **countdown honesto** ("tente em {n}s", botão Retry desabilitado até zerar) p/ `scope:'minute'` e "volta mais tarde" p/ `scope:'day'` (sem countdown falso); `openManual` agora **preserva o texto digitado** (vira descrição do gasto via `setAssistantQuickAddDraft`, amount 0 ignorado pelo QuickAdd). i18n `error.rate_limited_wait`/`retry_in`/`ai_unavailable_day` pt/en/es.
  - **(FB-19 · DEC-272) "00000000" nomeado, não dropado.** Sentinel `SYSTEM_INSTALL_ID` + `isSystemInstallId` no worker; `aiUsage().topUsers` marca `isSystem` (o all-zeros = sonda/scanner mandando placeholder no `X-Install-Id`). O Admin **nomeia** "Sistema / sonda (00000000)" (amarelo, fora do ranking de usuários reais) — o consumo segue contabilizado e VISÍVEL (decisão do Julio: entender a origem, não só apagar).
  - **(FB-17 · DEC-272) Tokens por função por usuário.** Endpoint DO `GET /install-detail?id=` (`SELECT fn,SUM(tokens),SUM(runs) … GROUP BY fn`); o modal do usuário busca no mount e mostra Assistente/Nota/Transcrição com tokens + chamadas.
  - **(FB-21 · DEC-274) Erros + navegador por usuário.** Coluna `browser` em `installs` (migração idempotente `ALTER TABLE … ADD COLUMN` em try/catch; capturada no ingest via `telemetryStr`, enviada pelo cliente com `deviceBrowserFamily()` — família grossa, nunca o modelo). Endpoint DO `GET /install-errors?id=` (join `error_seen→errors`); o modal mostra navegador + os erros (já scrubbed, sem valores) daquele install.
  - **(FB-18 · DEC-273) Governança Groq (server-side, real).** Endpoint DO `GET /ai-governance`: rollups de **hoje** + **mês** (tokens/runs), `byFnToday`, `activeToday` (distinct installs sem o sentinel) e os **limites REAIS** do free tier (`GROQ_FREE_LIMITS`, VERIFICADO 2026-06-22) computados do ledger `ai_usage` existente (**hot path intocado**). Domínio PURO `domain/admin/ai-governance.ts` (9 testes): `usagePct` (clampa 0–100, 0 p/ limite desconhecido), `fnUserCapacity` (RPD ÷ média req/usuário, floor, null sem sinal), `projectActiveUserCapacity` (menor capacidade = gargalo). `GovernanceSection` no Admin: KPIs do dia/mês, % do RPD por função (barra muda de cor ≥60%/≥90%) e projeção honesta "~N usuários ativos cabem antes do 1º teto". _(Snapshot dos headers `x-ratelimit-remaining-*` ao vivo fica como follow-up — a contabilização real já é server-side via `recordAiUsage`.)_
  - **Nada removido (ÂNCORA 9).** Schema só GANHOU `installs.browser` (migração aditiva). Caminho feliz da IA inalterado (só o ramo 429 mudou de forma).

  - **(FB-10 · DEC-258, M1) OCR estruturado + parser defensivo.** O prompt do worker `/ocr` passou a pedir, além de itens/total, **`date`** (sempre `YYYY-MM-DD`, convertendo qualquer formato impresso — nunca inventa), **`place`** (cidade/local impresso) e **`category` por item** (vocabulário fixo EN: bar/restaurant/market/transport/outing/entertainment/health/accommodation/other). `parseReceiptResponse` ganhou: `coerceReceiptDate` (só aceita `YYYY-MM-DD` **real** — rejeita `20/06/2026`, `2026-13-40`, `2026-02-30`), `resolveItemCategory` (confia na categoria do modelo SÓ se mapeia na taxonomia via `resolveCategory`; senão cai no `guessCategory` heurístico — modelo vence quando válido, `other`/lixo → heurística), e `placeLabel` agora prefere o `place` lido pelo modelo (fallback p/ `extractCity(merchant)`). Novo campo `ReceiptPlan.purchaseDate`. **Backward-compatível**: front antigo ignora os campos novos; worker novo só ADICIONA chaves.
  - **(FB-10 · DEC-258, M2) `commitReceipt` grava data + local como um gasto manual.** Helper PURO `receiptDateToIso(ymd)` converte o dia da nota → ISO **meio-dia UTC** (dia estável cross-timezone; nota date-only não tem hora real) ou null→"agora". `CommitReceiptInput` ganhou `purchaseDate?`/`place?`; cada linha da nota é carimbada com a data (uma vez, fora do loop — perf) e os campos de lugar via `placeToTransactionFields`. Data inválida/ausente → fallback "agora" (sem crash). Assim a nota cai no **dia em que aconteceu**, com o **lugar certo**.

O commit passa `purchaseDate`/`place` do plano. **Foto-na-IA** (`useAssistant.scanReceiptPhoto`): o intent `log_expense` agora carrega `place: plan.placeLabel` e `date: plan.purchaseDate` (o `resolveDate` repassa `YYYY-MM-DD` direto) → a foto resumida na IA também cai no dia/lugar certos. i18n `receiptScan.ai_details_title`/`date_label`/`place_label`/`place_placeholder` pt/en/es.

Country vem de `CF-IPCountry` (geo de borda) — correto. Worker redeployado `22449272`.
- **Fix CORS (worker · pego no 1º acesso real ao /admin pelo navegador)**: o painel (origin `pages.dev`) chama o worker (`workers.dev`) com header `Authorization` → preflight falhava (`Access-Control-Allow-Headers` não listava `Authorization`) e a tela mostrava "não foi possível atualizar os dados". `curl` (server-to-server) nunca dispara CORS, por isso passou nos smokes. Fix: `Authorization` adicionado ao allowlist de headers; respostas já carregavam `Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *` via `relayDoResponse`. Verificado: preflight OPTIONS retorna os 3 allow-headers; token novo→200, antigo→401. Worker final `6764c357`. (Token de admin **rotacionado** a pedido do Julio via `wrangler secret put` — valor não vai pro repo.)

- **🟢 0.99.35 (web/OTA + WORKER TelemetryStore) — Painel de Administração v1 · telemetria de uso anônima (nome SIM, valor NUNCA) + tela `/admin`.** Source: Julio — "um painel inicial de administração: quem está usando, com que frequência, quais funções, quantas compras dividiu/cadastrou, última vez que usou — sem dados sensíveis" + calibração: "quero NOME de quem usa e quanto usa, só não passar VALORES; consentimento on-by-default com opção de desativar; enviar ao abrir o app". **DEC-248.** **Arquitetura planner-na-nuvem/dados-no-servidor mínima (heartbeat, não event-stream):** (1) **Worker** ganhou o Durable Object **`TelemetryStore` (SQLite, migration `v5`)** + `POST /t` (ingest) + `GET /admin/*` (overview/installs/timeseries, **bearer `ADMIN_TOKEN`**) + `DELETE /admin/install` (limpar lixo/teste). Tabelas `installs` (1 linha/device: nome+contadores+flags+meta+`active_days`) e `heartbeats` (`install_id`+`day` → DAU/WAU/MAU). **Guarda de privacidade no servidor:** ingest só aceita chaves do allowlist (`TELEMETRY_COUNTERS`/`FLAGS`) — qualquer chave fora (ex.: `totalSpent`) → **HTTP 400**, então um valor monetário NUNCA é persistido. (2) **Cliente:** `domain/telemetry` PURO (`buildTelemetryPayload`/`deriveTelemetryFlags`/`shouldSendHeartbeat`/`utcDayKey`, clampa contadores, corta nome, dia UTC) + boundary `utils/telemetry.ts` (junta counts dos repos, nome do dono via `Participant.isOwner`, POSTa `/t` **≤1×/dia UTC**, best-effort/silencioso, respeita opt-out e pula demo) + `utils/telemetry-events.ts` (tally leve p/ `aiEntries`/`receiptScans`/`crashes` sem puxar o grafo de repos). Heartbeat disparado no `RootLayout` (app open + volta ao foreground). (3) **`AppSettings.telemetryEnabled` default true** (on-by-default) + toggle nas Configurações + aviso i18n pt/en/es ("dados anônimos p/ melhorar — nunca valores nem conteúdo dos gastos"). (4) **Painel `/admin`** (rota standalone, fora do shell, token-gated): KPIs (total de usuários desde sempre, DAU/WAU/MAU, novos 7d), barras de uso por funcionalidade, adoção (% por flag), distribuições (plataforma/versão/país) e **tabela de usuários com NOME + última vez + dias ativos + versão/plataforma/país + viagens/gastos/divisões/IA + remover**. (1) **Picker de amigos no hub de cobrança:** `SharedExpensesPage` (Acerto de contas) ganhou, no form "adicionar pessoa", a lista "Amigos conectados" (`availableFriends` = peerLinks conectados que ainda NÃO são pessoa da viagem) → `handleAddFriend` adiciona o amigo como participante da viagem num toque via `pairParticipantFromIdentity` (mesma plumbing do split, sem digitar nome/QR). (2) **Reconectar aparelho novo:** núcleo PURO `domain/connections/connections.ts` → `findReconnectCandidate` só sugere quando (a) o participante TEM `linkedActorId`, (b) o link atual está **offline/ausente**, (c) existe **exatamente 1** candidato novo com `publicKey` + `displayName` de nome normalizado idêntico (`normalizeName`: sem acento/caixa) e `actorId` diferente — nunca adivinha em ambiguidade. O orquestrador `reconnectParticipantDevice` (sync-orchestrators) é **neutro no ledger**: só repõe `linkedActorId`, remapeia o `peerLink` novo p/ o participante e aposenta (soft-delete) o link antigo — **as fatias/dívidas do participante ficam intactas** (provado por teste). UI: botão "Reconectar novo aparelho de {nome}" por participante (não-dono) quando há candidato. i18n `connections.reconnect_device`/`connections.reconnected` pt/en/es. **Nada removido (ÂNCORA 9).** No schema/worker change.

- **🟢 0.99.31 (web/OTA only) — Coerência do App · ÉPICO C2 — ponte Saída→Divisão ("dividir esta saída"): mover, não duplicar.** Source: Julio (backlog estrutural — "Saída vs Mesa ao vivo: clarear quando usar cada um; possivelmente um ponto de entrada 'sozinho ou em grupo?'") + conselho `app-coherence-project §C2`. **Decisão do conselho (Chair, contra o ponto-de-entrada):** NÃO perguntar "sozinho ou em grupo?" na ABERTURA (a saída é um fluxo solo de primeira classe, vivido durante a noite; a divisão é o ato de FECHAR a noite). Em vez de bifurcar a entrada, expor a divisão como **ação disponível no fim da saída** — o Advocate ganhou (atrito zero pra quem só quer marcar bebida; a divisão aparece exatamente quando a conta chega). O Critic levantou o risco real: **gasto contado em dobro** (a saída já é um gasto; virar split não pode somar de novo). **Fix (aditivo, ZERO rewrite do split — reusa o mesmo motor da nota):** (1) builder PURO `domain/split/from-outing.ts` → `buildSplitFromOuting` espelha `buildSplitFromReceipt`: cada rodada (`amountCents>0`) vira `SplitItem` com descrição/categoria preservadas; dono é o único participante; sem persistência/transporte. (2) slot in-memory single-use `features/split/outing-split-handoff.ts` (espelha `receipt-split-handoff`): carrega a `SplitSession` pré-montada + `supersedeOutingSessionId` + ids das txs; nada sai do device. (3) `OutingPage` (SessionReview) ganhou botão secundário **"Dividir esta saída com outras pessoas"** (só com rodadas) → monta o split da saída, seta o handoff e navega `/split/scan`. (4) `SplitPage` consome o handoff no mount (efeito único, espera `trip`), abre direto em `divide` e guarda o `supersedeOutingRef`. (5) **`commitSplit`/`undoSplitCommit` ganharam `supersededOutingSessionId`** — no MESMO `db.transaction`, `softDeleteOutingRows` aposenta a Session+txs+sessionItems da saída de origem (move, não duplica); o `undoSplitCommit` faz `restoreOutingRows` (volta exatamente como estava, revision++). `resumedRef`/`resuming` semeados também por `hasPendingOutingSplitHandoff()` → o handoff vence o resume da mesa anterior, sem flash de "Retomando…". **Nada removido (ÂNCORA 9).** No schema/worker change.

- **🟢 0.99.29 (web/OTA + WORKER `58ff0a0e`) — IA Quick Entry · MULTI-GASTO: uma frase, vários gastos (frase real do Julio que falhava).** Source: Julio 2026-06-21 — "essa frase não está funcionando na IA: 'Saí com o Bruno e a Débora, o Bruno me pagou um sorvete de 2, a Débora uma água de 1, dividi um bolo de 10 com ela, paguei 4 de estacionamento e comprei uma pizza de 10 que dividimos nós 3' — deveria criar uma LISTA de gastos com junções, quem pagou quanto e quem deve a quem; testar com muitas frases complexas até funcionar". **Root cause (comprovado):** o roteador da nuvem (DEC-246) só devolvia UM `AiIntent` e o cliente só parseava um → numa frase de 5 eventos só o último sobrevivia. **Fix (arquitetura multi-action, planner-na-nuvem / executor-no-device preservada):** (1) **Worker `/assistant`** agora retorna `{"actions":[ ... ]}` — UM `AiIntent` por evento de dinheiro, na ordem; prompt reescrito com a regra "quantas ações" (split de N pessoas = 1 ação, NÃO N), exemplo multi-evento de 5 ações e `max_tokens` 500→2000 (uma história não trunca o JSON). (2) **`domain/assistant/intent.ts`** — `parseAssistantIntents` (lista) + `extractCandidates` aceita `{actions}`/`{intents}`/`{intent}`/array nu/objeto nu/string JSON; `parseAssistantResponse` virou wrapper do 1º evento (back-compat). (4) **`useAssistant`** ganhou fase `batch_preview`, `runBatch`/`confirmAddPeople`/`confirmBatch` (commita cada op pelo mesmo `executeOp`, junta os undos num "Desfazer" único, toast combinado). (5) **`AssistantSheet`** ganhou `BatchPreviewArea` (lista de eventos + "minha parte" total + 1 confirm) e o ramo `add_people` no `ClarifyArea`. i18n `batch.*`/`clarify.add_people`/`done.batch` pt-BR/en/es. **Nada removido (ÂNCORA 9)** — fluxo single-evento intocado; multi só dispara quando há >1 evento.

- **🟢 0.99.28 (web/OTA only) — Coerência do App · ÉPICO B1 — captura unificada: ponte "Escanear nota → Dividir ao vivo" (1 motor de OCR, 2 portas).** Source: Julio (backlog estrutural — "Gastos→Escanear nota e Dividir conta são tão parecidos, mas não são o mesmo") + conselho `integration-overlaps-audit §2.1` (Chair: **manter 2 portas, unificar o motor por baixo, NÃO reescrever o split**; "depois do OCR, toggle Só meu↔Dividir"). **Achado (código):** `ReceiptScanPage` (`/receipt/scan`) e `SplitPage` (`/split/scan`) JÁ compartilham o MESMO OCR (`extractReceiptViaCloud`); o receipt já faz "só meu" + dividir-offline (DEC-208 picker). O único furo real era a ponte para a mesa **ao vivo**: uma nota lida em "Escanear nota" não virava pass-the-phone sem **re-escanear** pela outra porta. **Fix (cirúrgico, aditivo — zero rewrite do split, decisão alinhada ao Critic + mandato "sem regressão"):** (1) novo slot in-memory single-use `features/split/receipt-split-handoff.ts` (espelha `assistant-quickadd-draft.ts`: `set`/`take`/`hasPending`; carrega `ReceiptPlan` + a foto comprimida; nada sai do device). (2) `ReceiptScanPage` na revisão ganha card **"Dividir ao vivo"** (quando há itens incluídos) → seta o handoff e navega `/split/scan`. (3) `SplitPage` consome o handoff no mount (efeito único, espera `trip`) e abre direto na tela `divide` com a sessão pré-montada pela MESMA `buildSplitFromReceipt` que o scan manual usa — sem 2º scan; a foto segue para o anexo. `resumedRef`/`resuming` semeados por `hasPendingReceiptSplitHandoff()` → um handoff (nota nova explícita) sempre vence o resume da mesa anterior, sem flash de "Retomando…". **Decisão deliberada:** NÃO deletar `ReceiptScanPage` nem rotear tudo para o split (o "route-merge" total era o risco que o Critic vetou) — entregamos o resultado visível ("uma leitura, depois escolho só meu/dividir/ao vivo, mesmo motor") preservando os 2 engines estáveis e todos os testes. **Nada removido (ÂNCORA 9).** No schema/worker change.

- **🟢 0.99.26 (web/OTA only) — Coerência do App · ONDA 7 — FEAT F9: mesa ao vivo reconhece o usuário do app + registra a parte dele no próprio app.** Source: Julio 2026-06-20 — "quando compartilho uma mesa ao vivo com alguém que já tem o app, ele ainda pergunta o nome e faz o fluxo como se a pessoa não tivesse o app, e no fim ele não pega o valor com os itens que ela selecionou e pergunta se quer criar um gasto no app dela com o que selecionou, falando quanto deu e como ficou no orçamento dela". **Achado-chave (comprovado no código):** o `/t/:id` (mesa do convidado) vive FORA do BootGate mas DENTRO do `AppDataProvider` (RootLayout monta o provider acima de TODAS as rotas) → `useAppData()` funciona ali e carrega a viagem do PRÓPRIO device. Logo `trip !== null` é o sinal exato de "este convidado também é usuário do app"; `trip === null` é um estranho de verdade. **Decisão de arquitetura (conselho inline · Architect/Critic/Advocate):** NÃO transformar a fatia do convidado num débito do dono automaticamente (o dono é a fonte da verdade DEC-106; mexer nisso é risco MÉDIO-ALTO) — em vez disso, registrar a fatia como o gasto PESSOAL do próprio convidado no app dele, reusando os MESMOS orquestradores do quick-add, idempotente. **Implementado:** (1) **núcleo PURO `domain/split/guest-self-expense.ts` → `planGuestSelfExpense(...)`** decide o que oferecer pela fatia: `none` (sem fatia/viagem quebrada) · `no_trip` (estranho → porta de onboarding T9) · `already` (idempotente — já registrado) · `currency_mismatch` (moeda da mesa ≠ base da viagem → entrega pré-preenchido ao editor completo, onde mora a UI de câmbio) · `ready` (mesmo-moeda → registra direto, fase+pote resolvidos). Sem ids/FX/efeitos. (2) **F9a — reconhecer o usuário:** `SplitTablePage` lê `participants`/`trip`; se há um perfil dono local e o convidado ainda não se nomeou, **adota o nome do próprio perfil e PULA o prompt de nome** (não trata mais como estranho); a porta de onboarding T9 só aparece para `trip === null`. (3) **F9b — registrar a própria parte:** quando a fatia é positiva e há viagem, CTA "Registrar minha parte no meu app" cria UMA tx pessoal (`createExpenseTransaction`→`registerExpense`, `walletId:null`, `externalRef: splitguest:<shareId>`, `excludeFromLearning`) e mostra o **impacto no orçamento** pela MESMA `useSplitBudgetReading` do dono (copy `split.reading_*`); idempotência por `getGuestCommit/setGuestCommit` (mapa shareId→txId em localStorage) + o `externalRef` → reabrir o link nunca duplica (estado `already`). Moeda diferente → `setAssistantQuickAddDraft`+`/quick-add` (mesma escotilha do AI quick-entry). **Nada removido (ÂNCORA 9).** No schema/worker change (mesa ao vivo já trafega tudo que precisa; o registro é 100% local no device do convidado).
